*Bohao Wang,Chenwei Wu,Haoyu Li,Hang Zou,Yu Tian,Lina Bariah,Li Wei,Chongwen Huang,Yongliang Shen,Zhaoyang Zhang,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布 TelecomGPT-R1-9B，一个统一的开源电信领域推理模型。通过构建围绕协议、知识、建模、故障四个推理轴的 67,427 条 SFT 语料，并采用"多教师 LoRA-SFT + GRPO（DAPO 稳定化）"两阶段后训练，该模型在 GSMA 开放电信排行榜上位居开源模型首位，七轴均值与闭源前沿推理模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信是 LLM 推理的高杠杆领域，日常工程流程需要在规范性规范、运行遥测、厂商故障证据和精确的 RF/网络计算之间进行联合推理。然而，当前的 LLM 集成存在双面能力鸿沟：通用推理模型缺乏电信特定知识，而电信专用 LLM 在结构化、多步推理上受限。

Method: 1) 语料构建：策划 67,427 条 SFT 语料，围绕协议、知识、建模、故障四个互补推理轴组织，来源为轴对齐的公开 Web 资源，并通过轴特定 CoT 生成与前缀-续写自验证进行增强。2) 两阶段后训练（基座 Qwen3.5-9B）：第一阶段为多教师 LoRA-SFT，注入电信知识并诱导轴特定推理格式；第二阶段使用 GRPO，通过解耦裁剪和动态采样策略优化（DAPO）稳定化，基于四个轴对齐的二元验证器奖励优化策略。

Result: 在七个公开电信基准上，TelecomGPT-R1-9B 在开源电信 LLM 中排名第一，七轴平均成绩与最先进的闭源前沿推理模型相当，并在 GSMA 开放电信排行榜上位居榜首。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了"通用推理能力 + 轴对齐电信语料 + 两阶段强化后训练"这一路径能够弥合电信领域 LLM 的推理鸿沟，为开源电信专用推理模型确立了新标杆。

Abstract: Telecommunications is a high-leverage domain for large language model (LLM)-based reasoning because routine engineering workflows require joint grounding in normative specifications, operational telemetry, vendor-specific fault evidence, and exact RF/network calculations. However, current LLM integration in telecom remains bottlenecked by a two-sided capability gap: generic reasoners often lack telecom-specific grounding, while domain-specific telecom LLMs remain limited in structured, multi-step reasoning. To bridge this gap, we release TelecomGPT-R1-9B, a unified open-source telecom reasoner that ranks top-performing on the GSMA open telco leaderboard. Specifically, we curate a 67,427-example supervised fine-tuning (SFT) corpus organized around four complementary reasoning axes: protocol, knowledge, modeling, and fault. The corpus is built from axis-matched public web sources and enhanced through axis-specific chain-of-thought (CoT) generation and prefix-continuation self-validation. Starting from Qwen3.5-9B, we further develop a two-stage post-training recipe. First, multi-teacher low-rank adaptation (LoRA)-based SFT injects telecom knowledge and induces axis-specific reasoning formats. Second, group relative policy optimization (GRPO), stabilized by decoupled clip and dynamic sampling policy optimization (DAPO), optimizes the policy using four axis-aligned binary verifier rewards. Across seven public telecom benchmarks, TelecomGPT-R1-9B ranks first among open-source telecom LLMs and achieves a seven-axis mean comparable to state-of-the-art closed-source frontier reasoners.

</details>


### [42] [FIRSTPASS: A Multi-Domain, Multi-Round Peer Review Dataset Grounded in Real Editorial Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26129)
*Prabhjot Singh,Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FIRSTPASS是首个基于多学科高影响力期刊完整多轮编审对话构建的大规模同行评审数据集，包含来自Nature Communications透明同行评审的3,668条记录，覆盖生物学、化学、神经科学、物理学和地球科学五个领域，提供初始审稿报告、作者逐点回复、更新后评审意见的完整迭代结构，并带有基于编辑决策的STANDARD/EXTENDED结果标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学同行评审数据集仅基于计算机科学和机器学习会议构建，导致AI评审模型只会评判消融实验，却从未见过生物学家要求污染控制或化学家质疑核磁共振谱图归属。缺乏跨学科、带真实结果标签的高质量评审数据，限制了AI科学判断能力在多个学科领域的泛化。

Method: 从Nature Communications于2022年11月推行的强制性透明同行评审中筛选并构建数据集。每条记录包含完整的迭代验证结构：初始审稿报告、作者逐点回复以及更新后的评审者评估。结果标签直接来源于编辑决策（两轮评审为STANDARD，三轮及以上为EXTENDED），并采用自动化审计确保100%内容完整性。

Result: FIRSTPASS包含3,668条记录，横跨生物学、化学、神经科学、物理学和地球科学五个科学领域。专家评审平均长度2,155词，显著密集于会议评审。自动化审计确认100%内容完整性，且所有数据、解析流程和评估脚本均已公开。

Conclusion: FIRSTPASS填补了同行评审数据集的跨学科空白，提供此前所有语料库所缺乏的真实结果标签和完整多轮评审结构，为跨学科AI科学判断的可复现基准评测奠定基础。

Abstract: Scientific peer review datasets have trained AI systems exclusively on Computer Science and Machine Learning venues, producing models that critique ablation studies yet have never seen a biology reviewer demand contamination controls or a chemist question Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) spectral assignments. We introduce FIRSTPASS, the first large-scale peer review dataset built on complete multi-round editorial dialogues from a multidisciplinary high-impact journal. Curated from Nature Communications mandatory transparent peer review (instituted November 2022), FIRSTPASS comprises 3,668 records spanning five scientific domains (biology, chemistry, neuroscience, physics, and earth science), capturing the full iterative structure of scientific validation: initial referee reports, author point-by-point responses, and updated reviewer assessments. Each record carries an outcome label derived directly from editorial decisions (STANDARD for two-round review; EXTENDED for three or more rounds), providing ground truth absent in all prior corpora. An automated audit confirms 100% content integrity. Expert reviews average 2,155 words, substantially denser than conference venue reviews. All data, parsing pipelines, and evaluation scripts are released to enable reproducible benchmarking of AI scientific judgment across disciplines.

</details>


### [43] [Agents Don't Paginate: First-Chunk Selection for LLM Tool Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26130)
*Tatiana Petrova,Andrei Mazniak,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 面向LLM编码智能体（如Claude Code、Cursor、Codex等）的工具响应常超出单轮token预算，虽标准做法是分页，但在真实会话日志中从未观察到智能体主动请求第二页，因此第一页内容（即智能体实际阅读的部分）的第一项是否就是所需金标（precision-at-1，p1）成为关键。作者以0/1背包模型在500个SWE-bench任务上比较6种价值函数，并在5个模型上做了4,800次单轮文件定位调用。两个预注册假设均未成立：其一（核心负面发现）——提高p1并不能系统性提升下游准确率（各模型增量均<3个百分点且符号不一致、无显著差异），因为智能体能从第一页任意位置恢复金标；其二——在关键词打分器上增加四个文件元数据信号反而使p1下降4.8个百分点（p=0.001）。一个无参关键词打分器确实把p1从24.2%提升到35.0%（+10.8 p.p.，p=3.9×10⁻⁸），带原生排序兜底时达35.8%，但按核心发现这是仅提升rank-1、而rank-1恰是不影响最终答案的部分，故下游准确率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分页虽在协议层面普遍可用，但智能体从不请求第二页，导致第一页成为智能体信息获取的唯一来源。因此论文想弄清：把金标放到第一页首位（p1）是否真能改善智能体的下游定位表现，以及如何用可部署的轻量排序器提高p1——这既是对“分页被系统性忽视”这一工程观察的量化，也是对该假设的因果检验。

Method: 采用两类实验：(1) 离线受控基准——将第一页截断选择建模为0/1背包问题，在500个SWE-bench Verified任务上比较6种价值函数；(2) 单转文件定位探针——对5个LLM做4,800次调用，检验p1是否影响下游准确率。两个预注册假设分别检验“提高p1能否提高下游准确率”和“增加4种文件元数据信号能否提升p1”。

Result: 两个预注册假设均被证伪。核心负面结果：p1提升不会系统性提高下游准确率，各模型增量<3个百分点、符号不一致且均不显著，因为智能体从第一页任意位置都能恢复金标，起作用的是“金标被包含在第一页”而非其在页内排名。次要结果：加入4个文件元数据信号使p1下降4.8 p.p.（配对检验p=0.001）。正面工程结果：无参数关键词打分器把p1从24.2%基线提升到35.0%（+10.8 p.p.，p=3.9×10⁻⁸），加原生排序兜底后达35.8%，但不改变下游准确率。

Conclusion: “把金标排到第一页第一位”这一直觉虽能在离线指标上显著提升p1，却是rank-1层面、不影响最终答案的增益：智能体对第一页内排名的敏感度极低（<3 p.p.，方向不定），首要问题只是金标是否被截断进第一页。元数据信号叠加反而有害。因此优化方向应从“页内排序”转向“确保金标包含在第一页内”，并重新审视智能体对分页的不使用行为。

Abstract: Coding agents built on large language models (LLMs), such as Claude Code, Cursor, OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, and Aider, receive tool responses that routinely exceed the agent's per-turn token budget. The standard remedy, pagination, is available in every protocol that produced these responses; yet across the corpus of session logs from a public Model Context Protocol middleware we observed no agent-initiated requests for a second chunk. The first chunk is what the agent reads, so we ask how often the gold item (the one the agent needs) is placed first in it: the precision-at-1 rate $p_1$.
  In a controlled offline benchmark we treat first-chunk selection as a 0/1 knapsack and compare six value functions on 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks, then test whether $p_1$ matters with a single-turn file-localisation probe on five language models (4,800 LLM calls; not an end-to-end resolve-rate test). Two pre-registered hypotheses did not hold and are our main findings. The central one is negative: raising $p_1$ does not systematically raise downstream accuracy. Per-model deltas stay under three percentage points (p.p.), are not consistently signed, and no model is significant; the agent recovers the gold from anywhere in the chunk, so what reaches its answer is first-chunk inclusion, not the gold's rank within it. The second: adding four file-metadata signals to a keyword scorer hurts $p_1$ by 4.8 p.p. (paired significance test, $p = 0.001$).
  A parameter-free keyword scorer does raise $p_1$, from a 24.2% baseline to 35.0% (+10.8 p.p., far beyond chance; $p = 3.9 \times 10^{-8}$), and to 35.8% with a fallback to the tool's native ordering when no keyword matches. But by our central finding this is a rank-1 gain, and rank-1 is the part that does not reach the agent's answer: downstream accuracy does not move.

</details>


### [44] [Evaluating Language Models in Realistic Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26131)
*Ilija Subasic,Andrew Rabinovich,Zhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UPHELD 是一个基于真人撰写对话的大规模基准，包含数百个完整的人对人长对话、3万余个专家生成的对话轮次和3.6万余条逐轮人工标注，用于评估大语言模型在人类规模多轮对话中的质量。该论文发现传统自动指标和参考无关的LLM-as-a-judge方法与专家判断相关性差，并基于UPHELD提出Mixture-of-Judges框架，将多种评估信号结合，使与人类评估的相关性提升约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于开放、多轮交互中，但现有评估框架（如摘要、翻译、短问答）无法充分衡量人类规模对话的一致性，且现有指标的推导和验证常依赖合成数据而非真人来源。因此需要一个人类学基础的、超越事实正确性的长对话评估基准。

Method: 构建UPHELD：由专业编剧撰写数百个完整的人对人对话，具有真实的轮次密度；收集30000+专家生成轮次的36000+条逐轮人类标注。基于该基准系统评估经典自动指标与参考无关的LLM-as-a-judge方法，并开发Mixture-of-Judges框架，组合多种评估信号以贴近专家判断。

Result: 经典自动指标和参考无关LLM-as-a-judge方法与专家人工判断的相关性不可靠；而Mixture-of-Judges框架结合多种评估信号后，与人类评估的相关性提升了约30%。

Conclusion: UPHELD提供了一个稳健、以人类判断为根基的基准，填补了现有LLM数据集生态中缺乏人类规模对话评估的关键空白，是衡量人类规模对话智能的坚实基础。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to serve open-ended, multi-turn interactions, evaluating conversational quality at human scale has become a central challenge. Existing evaluation frameworks built for summarization, translation, or short-form QA tasks fall short of adequately measuring the consistency of human-scale dialogue, especially when derivation and validation of these metrics themselves often rely on synthetic rather than human sources. We fill the gap by introducing UPHELD (UPwork Human-Scale Evaluated Long Dialogues), a large, reference-full benchmark for evaluating human-scale conversational ability beyond factual correctness. UPHELD consists of hundreds of complete human-to-human dialogues authored by professional script writers, with realistic turn densities and 36,000+ per-turn human annotations across 30,000+ expert-generated dialogue turns. Using UPHELD, we systematically evaluate classical automatic metrics and reference-free LLM-as-a-judge approaches, and find them unreliable when correlated with expert human judgment. Building off this analysis, we use UPHELD to develop a Mixture-of-Judges framework that combines multiple evaluative signals and improves correlation with human assessments by approximately 30%. Overall, UPHELD provides a robust, human-grounded foundation for evaluating human-scale conversational intelligence that fills a crucial gap in the pre-existing LLM dataset landscape.

</details>


### [45] [Agent Seer: Synthesizing Scenarios from Specification Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26133)
*Harish Karumuri,Mahesh Vemula,David Lopes Pegna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Agent Seer，一种仅从单个 MCP 工具规范（无需示例、无需实时工具访问、无需领域调优）自动合成真实评估场景的流水线。它通过丰富原始 schema、生成带合成工具输出的分级场景，并扩展为基于 mock 数据的多轮对话，从而评估使用外部工具的 AI 智能体。在七个 MCP 规范上的评测显示其在工具调用正确性与对话连贯性上均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工构建工具调用评估场景需要深度领域专业知识、难以跨工具生态规模化，且生成的静态基准无法跟踪不断演进的 API。作者观察到工具规范（函数名、自然语言描述、类型化参数 schema）已蕴含足够语义信息，无需人工整理或真实工具执行即可合成逼真的评估场景。

Method: 流水线包含三个阶段：(1) 丰富原始 schema；(2) 生成带合成工具输出的分级评估场景；(3) 将这些场景扩展为有 mock 数据支撑的多轮对话。整个流程以单一 MCP 规范为输入，不依赖示例、实时工具或领域定制。评估采用七个跨领域、规模不同的 MCP 规范，衡量工具调用正确性与对话连贯性。

Result: 流水线在所有领域均达到高质量，中小型规范实现完全工具覆盖。两项发现：(1) 参数 schema 复杂度是质量变化的最强相关因素，工具套件规模影响较小且相对正交；(2) 参数值准确性是不完美场景中的主要失败模式——这是粗粒度名称匹配指标无法观测的子维度。

Conclusion: 工具规范本身足以作为无人工干预、可持续演进评估生成的语义来源；评估指标需超越名称匹配，关注参数值的细粒度准确性。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents that use external tools requires realistic test scenarios that capture how practitioners compose tools and iterate across conversation turns. Constructing such scenarios by hand demands deep domain expertise, does not scale across tool ecosystems, and produces static benchmarks that cannot track evolving APIs. We observe that tool specifications -- function names, natural-language descriptions, and typed parameter schemas -- already encode sufficient semantic information to synthesize realistic evaluation scenarios without manual curation or live tool execution. Agent Seer builds off this latent information: from a single Model Context Protocol (MCP) specification, with no examples, no live tool access, and no domain-specific tuning. This pipeline enriches raw schemas, generates graded scenarios with synthetic tool outputs, and expands them into mock-data-grounded multi-turn dialogues that exhibit strong tool-calling correctness and conversational coherence.
  Evaluation quality is measured by applying this pipeline on seven MCP specifications spanning diverse domains and tool-suite sizes and measuring the tool-calling correctness and conversational coherence. The pipeline achieves strong quality across all domains, with complete tool coverage on small and medium specifications. Two findings emerge within this analysis: parameter schema complexity is the strongest correlate of quality variation -- tool-suite size plays a smaller, orthogonal role -- and argument value accuracy is the dominant failure mode among imperfect scenarios, a sub-dimension invisible to coarse-grained name-match metrics.

</details>

Abstract: We present a modular data-science pipeline for estimating public sentiment towards individuals from fragmented, unstructured open-source intelligence (OSINT). Applied to public figures with known reputational outcomes, MINOS gives the clearest separation between positive, ambiguous, and negative cases. The results show that chained NLP and OSINT methods can support transparent, reproducible, human-in-the-loop sentiment assessment for high-stakes decision support. We demonstrate the approach on the UK Honours system, where individuals are required to display high standards of public conduct to maintain an Honour.

*Tanvi Nagilla,Alexander Jameson,Daniel Manta,Shayaan Uddin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"奖励知情稀疏自编码器"(RI-SAE)：通过GRPO轨迹的奖励高低将推理数据分为"好/坏"两组，训练标准JumpReLU SAE来检验奖励信号能否分离出可解释的推理特征。在Llama-3.1-8B上，所选特征确实能高区分两类（silhouette 0.79 vs 全编码0.005），但对照实验表明分离主要反映"解答完整性"而非"推理质量"——TF-IDF即可做到AUC 0.75–0.83，长度、闭合推理块、框选答案等三个结构线索即达AUC 0.70，且未见奖励的普通SAE完全无法分离（silhouette 0.01）。因此奖励筛选虽是一种廉价无监督的RL信号复用方式，但其主要内容为"完成形式"；仅有少数特征（符号数学；程序性/评价性语言）可读，作为示例而非孤立推理证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是让SAE更具针对性地"瞄准推理"：与其在通用激活上训练，不如利用强化学习（GRPO）天然产生的奖励信号来筛选数据，从而低成本、无需人工标注地复用RL信号服务于可解释性，并考察这种数据策展方式究竟测量了什么。

Method: 方法包括三部分：(1) 构建RI-SAE——按GRPO轨迹奖励将推理延续分为高奖励("good")与低奖励("bad")两组，在其激活上训练标准JumpReLU SAE（16,384个特征）；(2) 分析good/bad分离在特征层面的测量效果；(3) 设计对照实验组：TF-IDF文本分类器、三类结构线索（长度、闭合推理块、框选答案）、以及未见奖励的通用SAE作为消融。

Result: 结果：所选特征子集在Llama-3.1-8B上强烈分离两类（silhouette 0.79 vs 全编码的0.005），但该分离本质上是"解答完整性/完成形式"而非推理质量——TF-IDF已达AUC 0.75–0.83；仅三个结构线索即达AUC 0.70（99%的好完成vs 69%坏完成是框选答案的）；未见奖励的通用SAE无分离能力（silhouette 0.01，无可判别特征），证明0.79是对被策展信号的样本内拟合。仍有2个可读判别特征（符号数学；程序性与评价性语言）。

Conclusion: 结论：奖励筛选是廉价、免标签复用RL信号做可解释性的可行配方，但大多数被凸显的内容是"完成形式"（长度、结构、框选答案）而非推理质量；因此作者将配方与其对照测试配套发布，强调此类方法需要控制实验来排除表面结构线索，且可读特征仅视为示例证据。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose language-model activations into sparse, interpretable features, and an appealing way to aim them at reasoning is to curate their data with a signal reinforcement learning already produces: the reward. We build such a reward-informed SAE (RI-SAE): we split GRPO trajectories into high-reward ("good") and low-reward ("bad") reasoning continuations, train a standard JumpReLU SAE on their activations, and then ask what the resulting good/bad separation actually measures. On Llama-3.1-8B a sparse subset of the 16,384 features does separate the classes (silhouette 0.79 on the selected features versus 0.005 for the full code), but a control battery shows the separation is largely solution completeness rather than reasoning quality: a TF-IDF text classifier already splits the classes (AUC 0.75--0.83), and three structural cues alone (length, a closed reasoning block, and a boxed answer) reach AUC 0.70 (99% of good versus 69% of bad completions are boxed). A generic SAE that never saw the reward does not separate the classes at all (silhouette 0.01, no discriminative features), so the 0.79 is in-sample fitting of this curated signal rather than structure that a reward-blind dictionary recovers. We therefore present the recipe and its control battery together: reward filtering is a cheap, label-free way to reuse RL signals for interpretability, but most of what it surfaces is completion form. Two discriminative features are still readable (symbolic mathematics; procedural and evaluative language), which we take as illustrative rather than as isolated reasoning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L2英语学习者很难找到练习说话的对象，而口语又是最易引发焦虑的技能，因此自动化口语练习与打分市场增长迅速。然而自动评分只有在准确、可解释、公平且对标正确的人类标准时才可信。已有系统往往拟合人工标签或在透明度与公平性上不足，亟需一个不拟合人工标签、可解释且严格对照人类评分员的测评框架。

Method: 构建可解释的“特征加LLM”混合评分系统：确定性De Jong语音计时复合指标与单一文本LLM流利度判断进行融合。评估时不拟合任何人工标签，直接对照ICNALE全球评分档案（140段演讲、约80名训练评分员、10个分析标准），对130段含可用音频的演讲打分。通过配对bootstrap置信区间、两种一致的学习者隔离方法、独白负对照、经典测量值的逐特征复现以及按L1的分组公平性审计来支撑所有结论。此外设计了受控零假设实验：固定LLM与学习者文本，仅改变停顿在提示词中的写入方式（内联停顿位置vs.聚合停顿统计、中间小句标准）。

Result: De Jong语音计时复合指标达rho=0.764；与单一文本LLM流利度判断融合后达rho=0.818，优于80名个体评分员中的81%（高于中位评分员rho=0.73，接近最佳评分员），约为信度校正上限(kappa_max=0.99)的83%。混合相对纯复合指标提升+0.054（配对bootstrap 95% CI [0.017, 0.108]，不含0）。停顿编码对照实验显示：内联停顿位置不优于聚合统计（-0.069，CI [-0.15, +0.08]），基于中间小句的标准也无可靠增益，影响被限制在约±0.1 rho以内，说明流利度信号源自实测语音计时特征而非LLM提示中的停顿写法。

Conclusion: 特征加LLM的混合方案能在不拟合人工标签的情况下达到接近顶级人类评分员的共识一致性，兼具准确性、可解释性与公平性；LLM提供粗略流利度排序，由连续计量复合指标进一步细化。停顿表示方式的选择对结果无可靠影响，验证了信号来源为语音实测特征。作者通过多重稳健性检验（两种学习者隔离法、CI、负对照、逐特征复现、L1公平性审计）为每项声明提供了充分证据，论证了自动化口语评分可信赖的评测范式。

Abstract: Second-language (L2) English learners can rarely rehearse speaking with a partner. Speaking is also the most anxiety-laden skill. These gaps drive a fast-growing market for automated speaking practice and scoring. But an automated score is trustworthy only if it is accurate, interpretable, fair, and benchmarked against the right human bar. We build an interpretable feature-plus-LLM hybrid for spontaneous L2 dialogue. We evaluate it without ever fitting to the human labels, against the ICNALE Global Rating Archive: 140 speeches rated by ~80 trained raters on 10 analytic criteria. We score the 130 L2 speeches with usable audio. A deterministic De-Jong speech-timing composite reaches rho=0.764. Blended with a single text-LLM fluency judgment, it reaches Spearman rho=0.818 against the consensus gold. This agrees with the consensus better than 81% of the 80 individual trained raters: above the median rater (rho=0.73) and near the best, and at ~83% of the reliability-corrected maximum (kappa_max=0.99). The blend improves on the composite alone by +0.054 (paired-bootstrap 95% CI [0.017, 0.108], excludes 0); the LLM adds a coarse fluency ranking that the continuous composite refines. We also report a controlled null on pause encoding, bounded to effects below about +/-0.1 rho at this sample size. Holding the LLM and learner words fixed and varying only how pauses are written into the prompt, inline pause locations do not beat aggregate pause statistics (-0.069, CI [-0.15, +0.08]), and a grounded mid-clause criterion gives no reliable gain. The fluency signal comes from the measured speech-timing features, not from how pauses are written for the LLM. We back every claim with two agreeing learner-isolation methods, paired-bootstrap CIs, a monologue negative control, per-feature reproduction of classical measurements, and a per-L1 fairness audit.

</details>


### [49] [Cross-Platform Generalisation Failure in Mental Health Natural Language Processing: A Five-Axis Fairness Audit of Transformer Models on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26138)
*Rajveer Singh Pall,Sameer Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出五轴审计框架CPFE（鉴别性能、校准、统计显著性、预测公平性、归因稳定性），在对四个Transformer模型进行跨平台评估后发现显著性能退化：AUC在Reddit上下降30.3-35.4%、Twitter上下降37.9-39.5%，ECE校准误差显著上升；平台特定温度缩放可将平均ECE降低88.0%而不影响判别性能，证明故障模式可分离；预测公平性与归因稳定性亦呈现严重跨平台差异。作者主张将五轴跨平台验证作为异构环境中精神健康NLP系统的标准要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神健康NLP系统常部署于异构平台环境，但缺乏系统的跨平台验证方法。现有评估多聚焦单一平台内的性能，忽视了跨平台的数据分布差异导致的判别退化、校准失效、公平性偏差与可解释性漂移，亟需一个统一、多维度的审计协议来衡量模型在异构环境中的鲁棒性与公平性。

Method: 构建CPFE框架，涵盖五个审计维度：鉴别性能（AUC）、校准（ECE）、统计显著性（多随机种子）、预测公平性（差异指数DI、先验偏移调整DI、均衡几率）、归因稳定性（Jaccard词汇重叠）。对BERT、RoBERTa、Emotion-DistilRoBERTa、GoEmotions-RoBERTa四个模型在Kaggle精神健康语料（n=35,556）上训练，并在Reddit（n=6,257）与Twitter（n=2,883）测试集上评估，情绪标签映射为临床代理指标，另开展平台特定温度缩放与单种子微调对比实验。

Result: 四个模型中独立评估的三个模型均表现出跨平台AUC一致且显著退化（Reddit下降30.3-35.4%，Twitter下降37.9-39.5%，域内AUC为0.983-0.987），五个训练种子均确认。ECE由域内0.056-0.060升至Reddit 0.196-0.229与Twitter 0.499-0.542；平台特定温度缩放使平均ECE降低88.0%且判别性能基本不变（平均|ΔAUC|<0.01）。预测公平性方面，原始DI<0.17，先验偏移调整DI在Reddit为0.11-0.29，精神健康代理类别均衡几率差异达0.753-0.830（Reddit）与0.755-0.831（Twitter焦虑类）。归因稳定性上，K=10时16个模型-类别对中14对Jaccard J=0，词汇完全分歧。单种子微调平均AUC提升0.216，表明目标平台标签作为训练信号的收益大于作为校准信号。

Conclusion: 跨平台性能退化、校准失效、公平性失衡与归因漂移在多个模型与种子间高度一致且相互独立，证明异构环境中的验证必须覆盖CPFE全部五个维度。该工作将五轴跨平台验证确立为精神健康NLP系统在异构环境部署的标准要求，并提示目标平台标签应优先用于训练而非仅作校准。

Abstract: We introduce the Cross-Platform Fairness Evaluation (CPFE) framework -- a five-axis audit protocol covering discriminative performance, calibration, statistical significance, prediction equity, and attribution stability -- and apply it to four transformer models (BERT, RoBERTa, Emotion-DistilRoBERTa, GoEmotions-RoBERTa) trained on a Kaggle mental health corpus (n=35,556) and evaluated on Reddit (n=6,257) and Twitter (n=2,883) test sets with emotion labels mapped to clinical proxies. All three independently evaluated models exhibit consistent and substantial cross-platform AUC degradation (30.3-35.4% on Reddit, 37.9-39.5% on Twitter) relative to within-platform performance (AUC 0.983-0.987), confirmed across five independent training seeds. Calibration failure is concurrent and severe: ECE rises from 0.056-0.060 in-domain to 0.196-0.229 on Reddit and 0.499-0.542 on Twitter. Platform-specific temperature scaling reduces mean ECE by 88.0% without altering discriminative performance (mean |delta AUC|<0.01), confirming separable failure modes. Prediction equity analysis reveals large cross-platform disparities (raw DI < 0.17; prior-shift-adjusted DI: 0.11-0.29 on Reddit), with equalized odds differences of 0.753-0.830 for mental health proxy classes on Reddit and 0.755-0.831 for anxiety on Twitter. Attribution stability analysis shows near-complete vocabulary divergence across platforms (Jaccard J=0 in 14/16 model-class pairs at K=10). These findings support treating cross-platform validation across all five CPFE axes as a standard requirement for mental health NLP systems in heterogeneous environments. In a single-seed fine-tuning experiment, mean AUC improved by 0.216, suggesting target-platform labels provide greater benefit as training signal than as calibration signal.

</details>


### [50] [Syntax vs. Semantics: How Transformers Learn Deep Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26139)
*Jiangrui Zhao,Xiaoting Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个机制性框架，将大语言模型学习深层语义依赖的过程建模为“表面统计”与“深层语义”之间的竞争。理论分析发现“梯度饥饿”（Gradient Starvation）现象：早期优化中稀疏语义依赖的误差信号被主动抑制，阻碍结构推理的学习，并使其涌现表现为突发的相变。该框架还为思维链（CoT）的有效性提供了机制解释：CoT通过将中间推理步骤外化为具体token，绕过了隐式推理固有的抑制区。方法在玩具Transformer到生产级模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-Coder-7B）上得到验证；基于该理论提出的拓扑对齐对比目标可显式修正梯度几何，在变量绑定任务上相比标准交叉熵微调取得超过2倍的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然展现出显著的句法流畅性，但控制其获取深层语义依赖的优化动力学机制仍缺乏理解。作者旨在从机制层面揭示深层语义（结构推理）为何难以学习、其能力为何突现，从而为理解与改进推理能力提供理论根基。

Method: 1) 提出表面统计与深层语义竞争的理论框架；2) 从理论上识别“梯度饥饿”现象，指出稀疏语义依赖的梯度信号在早期优化中被误差竞争所抑制；3) 用该框架解释CoT为何有效（外化中间步骤以绕过抑制区）；4) 提出拓扑对齐的对比学习目标，显式修正梯度几何以解除对深层语义的抑制；5) 在玩具Transformer、Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-Coder-7B上进行跨尺度验证。

Result: 验证了梯度饥饿及结构推理以相变形式突现的理论预测；CoT有效性在机制层面得到解释；提出的拓扑对齐对比目标在变量绑定任务上相对标准交叉熵微调取得超过2倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: LLM语义能力的学习受梯度几何支配：表面统计抑制稀疏语义信号，导致结构推理延迟并突现式涌现；CoT通过外化推理步骤规避该抑制，而拓扑对齐的对比学习可直接修正梯度几何、显著加速深层语义获取。该框架为解释和增强LLM推理能力提供了统一的机制基础。

Abstract: Large Language Models demonstrate remarkable syntactic fluency, yet the optimization dynamics governing their acquisition of deep semantic dependencies remain poorly understood. We propose a mechanistic framework that models this learning process as a competition between Surface Statistics and Deep Semantics. Our theoretical analysis identifies a ``Gradient Starvation" phenomenon where the error signals for sparse semantic dependencies are actively suppressed during early optimization. This suppression impedes the learning of structural reasoning and causes its emergence to manifest as a sudden phase transition. Furthermore, this framework offers a mechanistic basis for the effectiveness of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) strategies. By externalizing intermediate reasoning steps into concrete tokens, CoT effectively bypasses the suppression regime inherent to implicit reasoning. We validate these findings across scales ranging from toy transformers to production models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B). Finally, guided by this theory, we propose a topology-aligned contrastive objective that explicitly rectifies the gradient geometry. Experiments on variable binding tasks demonstrate that our method achieves an improvement that is over 2x larger than that obtained via standard cross-entropy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [51] [Affix Cache for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26140)
*Kaihua Liang,An Zhong,Xin Tan,Zafar Ayyub Qazi,Hong Xu,Jian Weng,Marco Canini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（DLLM）中共享文本片段的高效推理问题，本文提出 ACache——一种基于"锚点词元"（Anchor Tokens）的仿射（前后缀）缓存复用机制：仅重计算约 20% 对生成结果影响最大的关键词元的 KV 状态，其余缓存直接复用，从而在恢复精度损失的同时，将共享前缀场景的延迟降低最多 55.7%、端到端吞吐量提升最多 1.68 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLLM 支持非自回归解码与双向上下文建模，但其高效推理面临独特困难：与自回归系统中共享前缀可重复利用 KV 缓存不同，DLLM 中共享上下文词元的 KV 状态与不断演化的生成词元通过双向注意力相互耦合，导致朴素缓存复用立即失效（stale），而完全重算开销巨大。因此需要针对 DLLM 特性的缓存复用机制。

Method: 提出 ACache：一种面向共享文本片段（不限于前缀，可包括后缀/中间片段，即 affix）的缓存复用机制。核心思路是：通过度量各词元对掩码（masked）生成词元的影响力，为每个请求筛选出少量"关键仿射词元"（Anchor Tokens），仅对这部分词元选择性重算 KV 状态，其余仿射词元的 KV 缓存直接复用。系统实现基于 Fast-dLLM，并另在 Nano-vLLM 引擎上搭建共享前缀原型进行验证。

Result: 在多种设置下，ACache 仅重算约 20% 的仿射词元即可恢复因直接仿射缓存复用带来的精度损失；在基于 Nano-vLLM 的共享前缀原型上，重计算延迟最多降低 55.7%，端到端吞吐量最多提升 1.68 倍。

Conclusion: ACache 证明：在 DLLM 中无需对所有共享上下文重算，只需精准重算少量高影响力"锚点词元"即可兼顾精度与效率，为扩散式大模型在共享上下文场景下的缓存复用提供了可行范式，并具备在现有推理引擎上落地的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) enable non-autoregressive decoding and bidirectional context modeling, but efficient inference remains challenging. Unlike autoregressive systems, whose key-value (KV) cache can be reused for shared prefixes, DLLMs couple the KV states of shared context tokens with evolving generated tokens through bidirectional attention, making naive cache reuse stale while full recomputation is expensive. We present ACache, an affix-oriented cache reuse mechanism for shared text spans in DLLMs beyond prefixes. ACache identifies a small request-specific subset of critical affix tokens, called Anchor Tokens, by measuring their influence on masked generation tokens, and selectively recomputes the KV states of only these tokens while reusing the remaining affix cache. Built on Fast-dLLM, ACache recovers the accuracy loss caused by direct affix-cache reuse across different settings when recomputing around 20% of affix tokens. We also build a shared-prefix prototype on top of the Nano-vLLM engine, showing that ACache reduces recompute latency by up to 55.7% and improves end-to-end throughput by up to 1.68$\times$.

</details>


### [52] [AdaThinking-E: One-Token Entropy Regulation for Adaptive Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26141)
*Zining Wang,Tongkun Guan,Boming Chen,Zhentao Guo,Jianqiang Liu,Chao Jin,Chen Duan,Kai Zhou,Pengfei Yan,Wei Shen,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AdaThinking-E，一个基于单token熵调节的强化学习框架，让多模态大语言模型自主学会"何时该深度思考、何时该快速作答"，从而在复杂问题上保持高精度、在简单问题上降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型将所有问题同等对待，一律启用深度推理，导致简单任务产生不必要的计算开销，既降低用户体验，又可能损害基准数据集上的准确率。因此亟需一种自适应思考机制，能够根据问题复杂度智能决定是否启用推理，且无需人工标注难度或外部干预。

Method: 提出AdaThinking-E强化学习框架，核心机制为"单token熵调节"。关键洞察是：模型对"是否进入思考"这一决策的置信度，可通过关键决策token上预测概率分布的熵来量化。据此设计熵驱动的奖励机制，使训练过程从高熵探索阶段（模型尝试不同思考策略）自然过渡到低熵收敛阶段（获得自信且可泛化的决策策略），从而让模型内在习得思考时机。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法使模型在多种文档任务上既能在复杂问题上保持高准确率，又能在简单问题上保持高效率，实现了精度与效率的兼顾。

Conclusion: 通过熵调节的奖励机制，模型能够无需人工标注或外部难度标签，自主发现何时该深度思考，为多模态文档推理提供了一种自适应、可泛化的高效决策方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong document reasoning capabilities by incorporating explicit thinking processes. While this capability significantly improves performance on challenging tasks, current models apply such deep reasoning uniformly to all questions, resulting in unnecessary computational overhead for simple task. This not only degrades user experience but also negatively impact accuracy on benchmark datasets. We identify the critical need for adaptive thinking mechanisms that can intelligently determine when to engage reasoning based on question complexity. To address this, we propose AdaThinking-E, a novel reinforcement learning framework that learns adaptive thinking through one-token entropy regulation. Our key insight is that model confidence in the decision to engage thinking (or not) can be quantified through entropy analysis of the predicted probability distribution at critical decision tokens. This observation motivates our entropy-governed reward mechanism: the training process naturally transitions from high-entropy exploration, where the model experiments with different thinking strategies, to low-entropy convergence with confident, generalizable decision-making policies. Crucially, this approach enables models to intrinsically discover when to think without requiring manual intervention or external difficulty labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach enables models to be both accurate on complex problems and efficient on simple ones across diverse document tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Position Is All You Need: A Free Lunch Token Compression Strategy for MLLM-based Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26142)
*Yuhan Liu,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAYN，一种仅依赖位置信息、即插即用且无需训练的视觉 token 压缩方法，用于缓解基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的指代表达式分割（RES）任务中高昂的计算开销。作者发现 RES 任务中的 token 压缩必须保留原始位置嵌入与局部邻域空间结构，并据此设计 PAYN：在保留原始位置索引的前提下，于每个局部邻域均匀保留 token，以维持空间关系一致性。在多个 RES 基准上，PAYN 优于现有 token 压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然 MLLM 大幅提升了 RES 的性能，但其高昂的计算开销成为关键瓶颈且鲜有研究。现有典型 token 压缩方法在该任务上出现严重的性能退化，作者希望通过理解这一现象找到解决方案。

Method: 通过大量实验发现，RES 的 token 压缩需要保留原始位置嵌入和局部邻域空间结构，说明视觉 token 位置信息在此任务中远比在其他任务中关键。基于该洞察，提出 PAYN：一个纯依赖位置信息的即插即用、免训练 token 压缩方法。PAYN 在每个局部邻域中保留分布均匀的 token，并严格保留其原始位置索引，从而维持空间关系的连贯性。

Result: 在多个 RES 基准上的实验表明，PAYN 优于现有各种 token 压缩方法，验证了在基于 MLLM 的 RES 任务中，位置信息对于 token 压缩而言是充分且必要的。

Conclusion: 位置信息是 MLLM 基础 RES 任务中 token 压缩的关键所在；PAYN 作为一种简洁高效的免训练方案，可在不引入额外训练开销的情况下显著缓解计算瓶颈，代码已开源。

Abstract: Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) aims to generate pixel-wise segmentation masks from complex and implicit textual queries. While recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have substantially boosted RES performance, their prohibitive computational overhead remains a critical bottleneck, which, however, is rarely explored. To fill this gap, we first evaluate typical token compression methods on this task and observe a surprising performance degradation. In this paper, we aim to understand this phenomenon for a solution. By extensive experiments, we find that token compression for RES requires preserving the original position embeddings and local neighboring spatial structures, indicating that visual token position information is far more critical than in other tasks. Building on this insight, we ask: Can we design the token compression method purely based on the position information? Therefore, we propose PAYN, a plug-and-play, training-free token compression method that relies solely on position information. PAYN retains tokens that are adequately distributed in every local neighboring region while strictly preserving original positional indices, thereby maintaining spatial relational consistency. Experiments on multiple RES benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing token compression methods, verifying that position is indeed all you need for token compression in the MLLM-based RES task. Codes are avaliable at https://github.com/YuhanLiu231/PAYN.

</details>


### [54] [Beyond Accuracy: A Qualitative Analysis of Vision-Language Models for Hate Speech Detection in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26143)
*Muhammad Jawad Chowdhury,Adiba Hasan,Ishrak Hossain,Shahriar Ivan,Sabbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对仇恨表情包（hateful memes）这一复杂且依赖上下文的仇恨言论形式，本文对四种最先进的视觉语言模型（LLaVA-7B、Qwen-VL、GPT-4o mini、Claude 3 Haiku）进行了定性分析，在零样本与少样本提示条件下评估上下文框架如何影响模型输出，并深入分析模型生成的推理依据，以理解其识别仇恨表情包时的思维过程与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情包已成为传播社会与政治观点、甚至仇恨言论的强有力媒介，其匿名性和病毒式传播特性使其难以被识别。尽管视觉语言模型在多模态任务上表现出色，但它们往往忽略上下文、反讽等识别仇恨表情包所需的关键线索，因此亟需深入了解这些模型的判断过程与局限性。

Method: 选取四种先进的视觉语言模型（LLaVA-7B、Qwen-VL、GPT-4o mini、Claude 3 Haiku），在零样本（zero-shot）与少样本（few-shot）提示条件下进行评测；分析不限于简单的分类准确率，而是聚焦于模型生成的判别理由的定性评估，考察上下文框架对模型输出的影响。

Result: 论文以定性方式揭示了各模型在零样本与少样本条件下对仇恨表情包的判别倾向及其生成解释的差异，阐明了上下文框架如何改变模型输出，并识别出模型在处理复杂语境、反讽等微妙线索时的共同局限。

Conclusion: 仅依靠分类准确率无法充分反映视觉语言模型在仇恨表情包识别中的表现，定性分析其推理依据能更深入地揭示模型的思维过程与缺陷，为改进这类语境敏感任务的模型设计与评估方法提供依据。

Abstract: Memes have turned out to be a powerful tool through which individuals share their ideas concerning contemporary social and political problems. Their anonymity, as well as their ability to go viral, make them a powerful medium for spreading hate. It remains very difficult to identify such complex and context-dependent hate speech. Although they display excellent performance on multimodal tasks, vision-language models (VLMs) tend to ignore context, irony, and other subtle cues that play a key role in identifying hateful memes. In this work, we present a qualitative analysis of four state-of-the-art VLMs: LLaVA-7B, Qwen-VL, GPT-4o mini, and Claude 3 Haiku. We evaluate these models under zero-shot and few-shot prompting to examine how contextual framing influences their outputs. Our analysis goes beyond simple classification accuracy and focuses on a qualitative evaluation of the models' generated justifications, providing a more in-depth understanding of their thought processes and constraints when dealing with hateful memes.

</details>


### [55] [Why Current XAI Is Not Enough for Arabic NLP: A Critical Survey of the Explainability Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26144)
*Salima Lamsiyah,Ruslan Mitkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于阿拉伯语可解释人工智能（Arabic XAI）的批判性结构化综述。文章指出阿拉伯语NLP在三个层面缺乏解释：方法上过度依赖LIME、SHAP等少量事后解释技术；任务上集中于情感分析、仇恨/冒犯语言检测、假新闻与垃圾邮件等分类任务，对生成、检索、翻译、摘要等任务覆盖不足；语言学上很少解释阿拉伯语特有的形态学、附着词、方言变体、双言制、正字法歧义、变音符、语码转换、命名实体、文化指涉及古典/宗教语域等现象。作者提出一个涵盖任务、方法、语言单位、变体、目标与评估实践的 taxonomy，并倡导构建以语言学为根基的阿拉伯语XAI研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管XAI已成为NLP的重要主题，但阿拉伯语NLP在方法、任务和语言学三个层面都严重缺乏可解释性研究。作者认为阿拉伯语XAI不应只解释模型决策，而需要提供对阿拉伯语作为语言学、文化和社会技术对象具有忠实性的解释，因此有必要对现有文献进行系统梳理并提出研究方向。

Method: 采用批判性结构化综述方法，综合回顾跨文本、语音和多模态场景的阿拉伯语XAI文献，构建一个涵盖任务、方法、语言单位、语言变体、目标和评估实践的 taxonomy（分类体系），并据此提出研究议程。

Result: 揭示了阿拉伯语XAI在方法上高度依赖少量事后解释技术（LIME、SHAP、注意力可视化和显著性），在任务上集中于分类且主要面向情感分析、仇恨/冒犯语言检测、假新闻和垃圾邮件，在语言学维度上很少涉及阿拉伯语特有现象。据此提出了面向语言学根基的阿拉伯语XAI研究议程。

Conclusion: 阿拉伯语NLP不仅需要模型决策的解释，更需要忠实于阿拉伯语作为语言、文化和社会技术对象的解释。作者呼吁将更丰富的诊断性、反事实、探针、基于理由和以人为中心的方法引入阿拉伯语XAI，并填补任务与语言学层面的空白。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) is now a major theme in NLP; however, Arabic NLP remains under-explained in three connected senses. First, there is a method gap: Arabic XAI relies heavily on a small set of post-hoc techniques such as LIME, SHAP, attention visualization, and saliency, while broader NLP XAI offers richer diagnostic, counterfactual, probing, rationale-based, and human-centered methods. Second, there is a task gap: existing Arabic XAI work is concentrated in classification tasks, especially sentiment analysis, hate/offensive language detection, fake news, and spam, with weaker coverage of generation, retrieval, translation, summarization, structured prediction, and dialogue. Third, there is a linguistic gap: many explanations identify influential tokens, but rarely explain Arabic-specific phenomena such as morphology, clitics, dialectal variation, diglossia, orthographic ambiguity, diacritics, code-switching, named entities, cultural references, or Classical and religious registers. This critical structured survey synthesizes the reviewed literature on Arabic XAI across text, speech, and multimodal settings. We argue that Arabic NLP does not only need explanations of model decisions; it needs explanations that are faithful to Arabic as a linguistic, cultural, and sociotechnical object. We introduce a taxonomy of tasks, methods, linguistic units, varieties, goals, and evaluation practices, and propose a research agenda for linguistically grounded Arabic XAI.

</details>


### [56] [Vagdhenu: A Vrutta (Meter) Aware Shloka-to-Chant (TTS) System for Sanskrit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26146)
*Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Vagdhenu 是一个梵语诗律（vrutta）感知的 shloka 唱诵文本转语音系统，基于现成的 flow-matching TTS 主干和神经声码器，通过定制前端（经 Kannada 正字法避免 schwa 删除、遵循 sandhi 等精细音系）、诗律感知的参考片段匹配机制，实现高保真 parayana 唱诵；同时报告了"文本侧韵律条件器在自填充 flow-matching 主干中架构性失效"的负面结论，并在四种 TTS 家族对比中以五小时克隆数据达到约 4.6 的专家 MOS，已部署 1.8 万+诗节的语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语诗节（shloka）的吟诵唱诵（parayana）需要严格的诗律（vrutta/metre）与精细音系（如 visarga sandhi 及 jihvamuliya/upadhmaniya 同位音、送气对立、齿/卷舌/腭擦音区分）保真，而现有 Indic TTS 通常受 Devanagari 正字法引发的 Hindi 式 schwa 删除干扰，且缺乏诗律感知能力。作者旨在构建一个无需新架构、仅以现成组件组装即可高保真完成梵语唱诵的系统，并分享可复制经验。

Method: 采用现成的 flow-matching TTS 主干与大规模神经声码器，加上三方面组件：(1) 前端将梵语经由 Kannada 正字法路由，规避 Devanagari 在 Indic 模型中触发的 Hindi 式 schwa 删除；(2) 前端遵循梵语精细音系，包括 visarga sandhi 的 jihvamuliya/upadhmaniya 同位音、alpaprana 与 mahaprana 的送气对立，以及齿、卷舌、腭三类擦音保持区分；(3) 诗律感知机制：检测音律并从"半参考规则"下选择精确匹配的参考片段进行韵律引导。

Result: 报告了一个塑造系统的负面结果：在自填充 flow-matching 骨干中，文本侧韵律条件器在架构上是惰性的——模型从上下文 mel 恢复音高，嵌入得不到梯度，因此参考片段与 voice-steering 重训是仅有的有效韵律杠杆。横向对比四个家族（StyleTTS2、VITS2、Matcha-TTS 与所用 flow-matching 骨干），早期家族均在复合辅音或韵律上触及上限，而五小时克隆数据达到专家 MOS≈4.6。系统已部署两个产品：32 章、5183 诗节（约 17.5 小时）的视频语料，以及覆盖 12 部书约 18000 诗节的音频应用。

Conclusion: Vagdhenu 证明无需新架构，仅靠前端音系/正字法处理与诗律感知参考匹配，即可将现成 TTS 组件改造成高保真梵语唱诵系统；同时揭示了 flow-matching 骨干中文本侧韵律条件器的架构性局限，为后续研究者提供了经验教训（参考音频与 voice-steering 重训才是可用的韵律控制手段）。作者开源了前端、推理/训练代码、权重、单说话人唱诵数据集与交互式 demo，支持复现与扩展。

Abstract: We present Vagdhenu, a vrutta (meter) aware shloka-to-chant system for Sanskrit: a text-to-speech system that maps
  a metrical verse to its chanted parayana recitation at high fidelity. This is an experience report, not a new
  architecture. We take an off-the-shelf flow-matching TTS backbone and a large-scale neural vocoder, and add the
  components a faithful Sanskrit chant pipeline needs: a frontend that routes Sanskrit through Kannada orthography to
  avoid the Hindi-style schwa deletion that Devanagari triggers in Indic models; a frontend that obeys subtle
  Sanskrit phonology (visarga sandhi with its jihvamuliya and upadhmaniya allophones, the aspiration contrast of
  alpaprana and mahaprana, and the dental, retroflex, and palatal sibilants kept distinct); and a vrutta-aware
  mechanism that detects the meter and picks an exactly matched reference under a half-reference rule. We report a
  negative result that shaped the system: in a self-infilling flow-matching backbone, a text-side prosody conditioner
  is architecturally inert, because the model recovers pitch from the context mel and the embedding gets no
  gradient; the reference clip and a voice-steering retrain are the only working prosody levers. We also report a
  comparative lineage across four families (StyleTTS2, VITS2, Matcha-TTS, and the flow-matching backbone), where each
  earlier family hit a ceiling on conjuncts or prosody that a five-hour clone cleared at an expert MOS near 4.6. The
  system shipped two deployments: a 32-chapter, 5183-verse video corpus (about 17.5 hours) and an audio app covering
  about 18000 verses across 12 books. We release the frontend, inference and training code, weights, a
  single-speaker chant dataset, and an interactive demo.

</details>


### [57] [CARE: Causally-Aligned Reasoning Exploration for Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26147)
*Yucheng Zhou,Peng Luo,Qianning Wang,Chengzhong Xu,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE（因果对齐推理探索）框架，通过两个理论条件（因果充分性与近端可学习性）筛选高质量训练轨迹，并结合双流目标（在策略组相对探索+难度加权经验回放）来优化医学大语言模型的推理，缓解了基于结果的强化学习中“答案对但推理错”的问题，在医学多模态与文本基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学推理任务的专家标注数据稀缺且昂贵，限制了LLM的进步。虽然强化学习是可扩展的替代方案，但医学场景下基于结果的标准方法存在自回归信用分配失败和梯度方差爆炸问题，导致模型强化虚假相关与数据集捷径而非有效的临床推理（即"Right Answer, Wrong Reason"陷阱）。

Method: CARE框架包含：(1) Causal Sufficiency条件——利用基于一致性的自验证机制模拟do-演算干预，从而去偏梯度；(2) Proximal Learnability条件——通过动态熵界在模型的最近发展区内选择经验，实现方差有界的优化。筛选出的经验通过双流目标优化：在策略组相对探索（on-policy group-relative exploration）与难度加权经验回放（difficulty-weighted experience replay）相结合。

Result: 在多样化的医学多模态与纯文本基准上的大量实验表明，CARE持续优于其他强基线，显著减少了“答案正确但不一致推理”的现象，并提升了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: CARE通过理论驱动的经验筛选与双流优化机制，有效解决了医学推理中结果型RL的信用分配与梯度方差问题，为缺乏专家标注的医学领域提供了更稳健、可扩展的推理增强范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for medical reasoning, yet the scarcity and cost of expert-annotated data constrain their progress. While reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative, standard outcome-based methods in medicine often suffer from autoregressive credit assignment failure and gradient variance explosion. This leads to the "Right Answer, Wrong Reason" trap, where models inadvertently reinforce spurious correlations and dataset shortcuts rather than valid clinical deduction. In this work, we propose Causally-Aligned Reasoning Exploration (CARE), a theoretically grounded framework for intrinsic experience curation. CARE is built upon two rigorous conditions for high-quality training trajectories: Causal Sufficiency, which utilizes an agreement-based self-verification mechanism to mimic $do$-calculus interventions and effectively debias gradients; and Proximal Learnability, which employs dynamic entropy bounds to select experiences within the model's zone of proximal development for variance-bounded optimization. These rigorously filtered experiences are optimized via a dual-stream objective that combines on-policy group-relative exploration with difficulty-weighted experience replay. Extensive experiments on diverse medical multimodal and text-only benchmarks demonstrate that CARE consistently outperforms other strong competitors, substantially reducing correct-but-inconsistent reasoning and improving training stability.

</details>


### [58] [Towards Interpretable Depression Detection: Linking Acoustic Features to DSM-5 Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26148)
*Jonas Länzlinger,Katharina O. E. Müller,Burkhard Stiller,Bruno Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的"链接框架"，将语音声学特征（音高变化、停顿、语速）显式映射到DSM-5抑郁行为指标上，在本地 commodity 硬件上运行以保护隐私，并在DAIC-WOZ数据集上初步验证了声学特征与精神运动改变及注意力困难等指标的关联方向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症影响全球数百万人，但目前的诊断依赖主观自我报告，可能遗漏真实行为表现。现有黑盒模型缺乏可解释性，无法将语音信号与临床诊断标准（DSM-5）直接对应，同时云端处理也带来隐私风险。

Method: 提出透明的Linkage Framework：将语音声学特征（pitch variability音高变化、pauses停顿、speech tempo语速）显式映射到DSM-5抑郁行为指标（如精神运动改变、注意力困难），输出指标层级而非黑盒预测；系统本地运行在低配置硬件上以保障隐私，并考虑边缘计算约束。

Result: 在DAIC-WOZ数据集上的初步评估显示，声学特征与DSM-5指标（精神运动改变、注意力困难）之间存在方向一致的关联，支持了设计合理性。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了可解释、隐私保护型语音抑郁评估的可行性；未来工作将在纵向数据集上验证，并在保持边缘约束的前提下扩展多模态集成。

Abstract: Depression affects millions worldwide, yet diagnosis relies on subjective self-reports that may miss authentic behavior. This paper presents an approach linking speech acoustics to DSM-5 depressive-behavior indicators through a transparent Linkage Framework. Unlike black-box models, the framework explicitly maps acoustic features (pitch variability, pauses, speech tempo) to clinical indicators, enabling interpretable, indicator-level outputs. The system runs locally on commodity hardware (HW) to preserve privacy. Preliminary evaluation on DAIC-WOZ shows directionally consistent associations between acoustic features and DSM-5 indicators for psychomotor change and concentration difficulty, supporting the design rationale. Future work will validate on longitudinal datasets and extend multimodal integration while maintaining edge constraints.

</details>


### [59] [Artificial Intelligence Models Can Predict and Collaboratively Modulate Human Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26152)
*Eric Lacosse,Mariana Duarte,Graham Todd,Peter M. Todd,Daniel C. McNamee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在语义记忆搜索任务中能否作为认知工具扩展人类生成性认知能力，并通过经典语义流畅性任务（SFT）证明，LLM在追踪和预测人类记忆轨迹方面的表现优于其他人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往观点认为LLM会取代人类认知努力，但作者提出LLM更可能充当认知工具来扩展人类能力，尤其是在开放式概念探索与创造性构思任务中。然而，目前尚不清楚LLM在人类-AI交互中如何增强这种生成性认知能力，因此需要实证研究加以考察。

Method: 采用语义流畅性任务（SFT）这一经典认知范式，该任务要求生成性语义记忆检索，长期用于刻画人类聚合思维与发散思维。实验中比较LLM与人类在语义记忆搜索中追踪和预测人类记忆轨迹的能力。

Result: 结果表明，LLM在追踪和预测人类语义记忆轨迹方面的能力超过其他人类参与者。

Conclusion: LLM不是简单地替代人类认知努力，而是能够在人类-AI交互中增强人类的生成性认知能力；其在人类认知轨迹预测上的优势为LLM作为认知工具提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit unprecedented natural language generation and many text-based problem-solving capabilities. Indeed, in many language-based tasks, for example routine coding, these artificial intelligence models have reduced, or even eliminated, the need for human input. But rather than replacing human cognitive effort, LLMs may instead serve as cognitive tools to extend human abilities, particularly when they are engaged in a task requiring open-ended conceptual exploration and creative ideation. However, we are yet to understand how these models may enhance such generative human cognitive abilities in human--AI interactions. In this study, we explore and evaluate the ability of LLMs to follow and enhance human mental trajectories during semantic memory search. To test this, we use the semantic fluency task (SFT), a classic cognitive paradigm requiring generative semantic memory retrieval that has long served to characterize convergent and divergent thinking in humans. We demonstrate that an LLM's abilities to track and predict human memory trajectories in this task exceed those of other humans.

</details>


### [60] [Evaluating AI Generated Summaries for Cancer Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26154)
*Muhammad Aurangzeb Ahmad,Kim Shyu,Leon Oliver,Fergus Sleight,Paul Landau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在癌症患者护理应用中评估了由大语言模型生成的医学总结，采用了“人类专家评估 + LLM-as-a-judge”的双重评估框架。人类专家（肿瘤科临床医生和面向患者的护理人员）从准确性、临床相关性和可读性三个维度提供金标准评估，同时用LLM作为评估者进行并行评价。研究发现生成的总结存在偶发性遗漏和轻微不准确等问题，并基于这些发现对提示词设计、地面实况（grounding）和安全护栏进行了迭代改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被越来越广泛地集成到数字健康平台中，用于生成复杂医疗数据的摘要。虽然这些模型可以改善患者参与和沟通，但在临床环境中，其准确性、忠实性和安全性引发担忧，因此需要系统评估AI生成医疗摘要的质量。

Method: 采用双重评估框架：(1) 人类领域专家（肿瘤科临床医生和面向患者的护理人员）提供关于准确性、临床相关性和可读性维度的金标准评估；(2) 并行使用LLM作为评估者（LLM-as-a-judge）进行自动评估。发现的局限性通过系统性分析用于迭代改进提示词设计、grounding（将输出锚定在可靠数据源）和安全护栏。

Result: AI生成的总结存在一些局限性，如偶尔的信息遗漏和轻微不准确。通过系统的错误分析，这些局限被识别并转化为对生成流程的改进。

Conclusion: 通过将人类专家评估与LLM-as-a-judge相结合的双重评估框架，可以系统性地发现并修正AI生成医疗摘要的缺陷，提示词设计、grounding和安全护栏的迭代优化是提升临床环境中AI摘要质量与安全性的关键路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into digital health platforms to generate summaries of complex medical data. Although these models can improve patient engagement and communication, these systems also raise concerns about accuracy, faithfulness, and safety in clinical contexts. In this study, we evaluate AI-generated summaries within a cancer patient care application using a dual assessment framework. Human domain experts, including oncology clinicians and patient-facing care staff, provided ground-truth evaluations of summary quality along dimensions of accuracy, clinical relevance, and readability. In parallel, we employed LLMs serving as evaluators (LLM-as-a-judge). Some limitations were identified in the generated summaries e.g., occasional omissions and minor inaccuracies. These were systematically analyzed and used to iteratively improve prompt design, grounding, and safety guardrails.

</details>


### [61] [VFA: Empowering Multilingual MLLMs via Vision-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26155)
*Yixia Li,Yaqing Shi,Zhiwen Ruan,Dongdong Zhang,Lingjie Jiang,Shaohan Huang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉无关适配（VFA）框架，通过将多语言文本任务向量与视觉对齐任务向量在共享LLM主干上合并，解耦多语言能力增强与视觉对齐，从而以低数据成本提升多模态大语言模型的多语言能力，同时避免灾难性遗忘和视觉-语言对齐破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数多模态大语言模型以英语为中心，扩展多语言多模态指令微调受限于高质量非英语图像-文本监督数据的稀缺和高成本。虽然多语言文本数据充足，但直接进行文本微调会破坏视觉-语言对齐并导致灾难性遗忘，因此需要一种既能利用丰富文本数据又能保持视觉能力的有效方法。

Method: VFA框架在共享LLM主干上通过组合互补任务向量实现多语言增强与视觉对齐的解耦。具体做法是：先在基础LLM上用多语言文本数据微调得到多语言任务向量，再将该向量与MLLM的视觉对齐任务向量合并（task vector composition/merging），无需额外视觉监督。

Result: 在5个MLLM和6个多语言多模态基准上的实验显示一致改进，同时保留通用多模态和纯文本能力。仅使用不到2%的文本数据，VFA即可接近完全多模态训练模型的性能，展现出很高的数据效率。

Conclusion: VFA验证了通过任务向量组合在共享主干上解耦语言与视觉能力是一种数据高效且可行的多语言多模态增强路径，显著缓解了多语言数据稀缺和高成本问题，为多语言MLLM的扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have advanced rapidly, yet most remain English-centric, as scaling multilingual multimodal instruction tuning is limited by the scarcity and high cost of high-quality non-English image-text supervision. Although multilingual text data is abundant, naive textual fine-tuning can disrupt vision-language alignment and induce catastrophic forgetting. We propose Vision-Free Adaptation (VFA), a framework that decouples multilingual language enhancement from visual alignment by composing complementary task vectors over a shared LLM backbone. Specifically, we fine-tune a base LLM on multilingual text data to derive a multilingual task vector, which is then merged with the vision-aligned task vector of an MLLM. Experiments on five MLLMs across six multilingual multimodal benchmarks show consistent improvements while preserving both general multimodal and text-only capabilities. Moreover, using less than 2% of the text data, VFA narrows the gap to the fully multimodal-trained model, demonstrating its data efficiency.

</details>


### [62] [Self-Generated Text Recognition: Quality Heuristics, Cross-Task Transfer, and Downstream Bias in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26159)
*Jesse St. Amand,Callum Canavan,Sohaib Imran,Joseph Hewson,Aaron Lutz,Shi Feng,Puria Radmard,Lennie Wells*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM的"自生成文本识别"（SGTR）能力，即模型识别自身输出的能力。作者通过识别六种关键实验设计选择（operationalizations），调和了以往关于SGTR能力相互矛盾的结论。基于13-21个模型的评估发现，SGTR准确率随评估格式（成对vs.单个）、对话结构（用户标签vs.助手标签）和任务领域（编码vs.摘要）显著变化，并确认"质量启发式"（模型将文本归属为质量更高者）是主要混淆因素。研究发现，通过SFT提升一种评估配置下的SGTR能力可泛化到其他配置，但同时会导致模型在AlpacaEval裁判场景中偏好自身输出。作者得出结论：尽管存在混淆因素，部分模型确实具备实用SGTR能力，且该能力应在安全关键型AI应用中受到监测和考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGTR能力会使依赖LLM作为评估者或监控者的AI安全机制面临风险——模型可能识别出其他同模型副本的输出并做出有偏判断或直接串通。然而以往研究对当前模型是否具备显著SGTR能力得出矛盾结论，缺乏对实验设计差异的系统性解释，这阻碍了科学评估与安全治理。本文旨在调和对立证据，识别导致结果分歧的关键因素，并评估训练SGTR能力的安全后果。

Method: 作者提出"operationalizations"概念——指驱动不同结论的关键实验设计选择。具体方法包括：(1) 在13-21个模型上，跨六种操作化配置进行系统评估；(2) 将操作化维度分解为评估格式（成对比较vs.单个文本评估）、对话结构（候选文本放在用户标签vs.助手标签中）以及生成候选文本的任务领域（如编码vs.摘要）；(3) 分析"质量启发式"作为混淆因素的作用；(4) 通过监督微调（SFT）训练某配置下的SGTR，检验其向其他配置的泛化能力；(5) 在AlpacaEval框架中测试SGTR训练对模型裁判偏好（自偏好）的影响。

Result: SGTR准确率高度依赖实验设计：评估格式、对话结构和任务领域均显著影响性能，这解释了以往文献的分歧。质量启发式被证实是主导性混淆因素。SFT提升SGTR能力在一种评估配置下可泛化到其他配置；但SGTR训练同时使模型在AlpacaEval裁判中倾向于偏好自身输出。综合表明，尽管存在质量启发式混淆，部分模型仍具备实用性的SGTR能力。

Conclusion: SGTR是一种真实且可训练的能力，其测量受实验设计强烈影响；训练SGTR不仅影响识别能力，还会泛化地影响模型的自偏好行为，可能加剧基于LLM评估/监控体系中的偏倚与串通风险。因此SGTR应被纳入安全关键型AI应用的设计与监测考量之中。

Abstract: Self-Generated Text Recognition (SGTR)--the ability of an LLM to identify its own outputs--poses risks to AI safeguards that rely on LLMs as evaluators or monitors. Specifically, an LLM may recognize outputs from other copies of the same model and make biased judgments or collude outright. Prior work has drawn conflicting conclusions about whether current models possess significant SGTR capabilities. We reconcile these findings by identifying key experimental design choices--which we term operationalizations--that drive divergent results. Evaluating 13-21 models across six operationalizations, we find that accuracy varies substantially with evaluation format (pairwise vs. individual assessments of text), conversation structure (presenting candidate text in user tags vs. assistant tags), and the domain of the task used to generate candidate text (e.g., coding vs. summarization). We corroborate previous observations that a quality heuristic--models attributing authorship to text they perceive as higher quality--is a dominant confound. We also find that improving a model's SGTR performance via SFT in one evaluation configuration can generalize to others. Training for SGTR additionally causes models to prefer their own outputs when acting as a judge in the AlpacaEval framework. Finally, we discuss the implications of our evaluations for the safety of future AI systems: our work suggests that, despite confounds, some models possess practical SGTR capabilities, and that training a model for SGTR in one setting can affect its self-recognition and self-preference more generally. We conclude that SGTR should be monitored and considered in the design of safety-critical AI applications.

</details>

### [63] [SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing for Labeled Property Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26132)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLM条件分层关系路由（SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing），将小型语言模型直接集成到图消息选择中。该方法由拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示作为预测锚点，随后对每个目标节点将邻接消息按关系类型组织，使用参数高效的小型语言模型处理结构化图软令牌并生成目标条件路由查询，先在每种关系类型内选择相关消息，再在关系级摘要间路由信息，最终对拓扑锚点施加有界残差更新，兼顾结构证据与上下文语义信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带标签的属性图将关系结构与节点和关系上的异构文本/类别属性相结合。传统图神经网络通常将这些属性表示为静态特征向量，难以根据具体预测目标确定哪些语义证据应影响消息传播，因此需要一种能让语言语义动态参与图消息选择的机制。

Method: 架构由三部分构成：1) 拓扑GNN提供稳定的结构表示与预测锚点；2) 对每个目标节点，将相邻消息组合为邻居结构状态、节点属性编码、关系属性编码和关系类型；3) 参数高效的小型语言模型处理结构化图软令牌并生成目标条件路由查询，该查询先在每种关系类型内选择相关消息，再在关系级摘要间进行跨类型路由，得到表示后对拓扑锚点施加有界残差更新。

Result: 该方法在邻居级和关系类型级均支持可解释分析，为将语言派生语义集成到属性丰富的图学习中提供了通用机制，同时有界残差更新保证了保留结构证据的同时允许上下文语义信息修正预测。

Conclusion: SLM条件分层关系路由克服了静态特征向量在属性图上的局限，实现了目标条件化、语义感知的消息选择与路由过程，并在结构基础与语言语义之间取得平衡，兼具可解释性与通用性。

Abstract: Labeled property graphs combine relational structure with heterogeneous textual and categorical properties attached to both nodes and relationships. Conventional graph neural networks typically represent these properties as static feature vectors, limiting their ability to determine which semantic evidence should influence message propagation for a particular prediction target. We propose SLM-Conditioned Hierarchical Relation Routing, an architecture that integrates a small language model directly into graph message selection. A topology GNN provides a stable structural representation and prediction anchor. For each target node, incident messages combine the neighbor's structural state, node-property encoding, relationship-property encoding, and relationship type. A parameter-efficient SLM processes structured graph soft tokens and produces a target-conditioned routing query. This query first selects relevant messages within each relationship type and subsequently routes information across relation-level summaries. The resulting representation provides a bounded residual update to the topology anchor, preserving structural evidence while allowing contextual semantic information to modify the prediction. The architecture supports interpretable analysis at both the neighbor and relationship-type levels and provides a general mechanism for integrating language-derived semantics into property-rich graph learning.

</details>


### [64] [NeuronFuzz: Safety Neuron Guided Fuzzing for LLM Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26222)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Muhammad Firhard Roslan,Joseph Gardiner,Sana Belguith,Lichao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NeuronFuzz，一个白盒模糊测试框架，利用模型内部安全神经元作为连续执行反馈，实现不依赖响应生成的 LLM 安全评估。它将安全神经元激活转化为可微分的连续安全警报分数（在 prefill 阶段即可获得），并通过梯度识别敏感模板位置、结合掩码语言模型生成流畅的上下文兼容变异，从而高效发现越狱攻击模板，且优化后的模板可零样本迁移到开放权重和专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化 LLM 安全测试依赖响应级反馈：每个候选提示都需要生成目标模型的完整响应来评估攻击有效性，成本高昂；并且对强对齐模型，大多数候选被拒绝且失败结果相同，反馈稀疏、信息量有限。

Method: 1) SafetyOracle：使用模板不变的有害/良性输入与稳定性感知选择，筛选出一组紧凑的安全神经元，将其激活转化为连续的安全警报分数，作为模糊测试反馈且在 prefill 阶段即可获得，免去响应生成。2) 由于安全分数可微分，利用梯度识别安全敏感的模板位置，并用掩码语言模型生成流畅、上下文兼容的变异，同时保留原始有害载荷并避免额外优化变量。

Result: 在 21 个文本和多模态模型上评估：在 5 个白盒源模型上实现 76-100% 的越狱发现率，比基线高最多 48 个百分点；优化模板可零样本迁移到开放权重和 6 个专有目标模型，平均 ASR/ensemble ASR 分别达 69.6%/92.6% 和 44.1%/60.0%。

Conclusion: NeuronFuzz 证明利用内部安全神经元的连续信号可以替代昂贵且稀疏的响应级反馈，显著提升越狱攻击发现的效率与覆盖率，并展示出跨模型的迁移能力，为 LLM 安全评估提供了一种白盒反馈驱动的新范式。

Abstract: Safety evaluation is critical for assessing whether aligned Large Language Models (LLMs) remain robust against jailbreak attacks. Existing automated testing methods, however, largely rely on response-level feedback: each candidate prompt typically requires generating a target-model response to evaluate its attack effectiveness. This process is expensive and, more importantly, provides only sparse guidance on strongly aligned models, where most candidates are rejected with the same failure outcome.
  This paper presents NeuronFuzz, a white-box fuzzing framework that exploits internal safety neurons as continuous execution feedback for LLM safety evaluation. A SafetyOracle converts safety-neuron activations into a continuous safety alarm score that serves as feedback for fuzzing and can be obtained during prefill, eliminating response generation from the fuzzing loop. To construct the SafetyOracle, NeuronFuzz uses template-invariant harmful and benign inputs and stability-aware selection to identify a compact set of safety neurons whose activations capture harmful-intent recognition. Moreover, since the safety alarm score is differentiable, NeuronFuzz uses its gradients to identify safety-sensitive template positions and a masked language model to generate fluent, context-compatible mutations while preserving original harmful payload and avoiding additional optimization variables. We evaluate NeuronFuzz across 21 text and multimodal models. Across five white-box source models, it achieves a 76-100% jailbreak discovery rate, outperforming baselines by up to 48 percentage points. Its optimized templates further transfer zero-shot to open-weight and six proprietary target models, achieving average ASR and top-5 ensemble ASR (EASR) of 69.6%/92.6% and 44.1%/60.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [65] [Mitigating Strong-Modality Collapse in Multimodal Learning via Inverted Asymmetric Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26879)
*Mary Ogbuka Kenneth,Foaad Khosmood,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“强模态坍缩（strong-modality collapse）”来解释多模态融合模型常无法超越最优单模态基线的原因：在对称注意力融合中，主导模态的内部精度会因融合而显著下降（如文本路径从74.9%降至56.4%）。为此提出倒置非对称融合（IAF），让主导模态不经改动地直接通过融合，次模态以其为上下文锚点做注意力；融合前再用模态感知知识蒸馏增强次模态。在MultiHuSE、UR-FUNNY和MUStARD上，IAF将主导模态内部精度保持在单模态上限，相比最强单模态基线最高提升8.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 期望多模态融合能提升性能，但在MultiHuSE等数据集上，早期、晚期和对称注意力融合往往无法超越最优单模态基线（文本）。通过路径隔离发现，对称融合会严重破坏主导模态的内部表示，这种“强模态坍缩”是融合失败的重要原因。

Method: 提出倒置非对称融合（IAF）：不再强制跨模态互注意力；主导模态以原始形式直接穿过融合层保持不变，次模态将其作为上下文锚点进行单向注意力；在融合前，通过模态感知知识蒸馏（Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation）增强次模态，以缩小模态间能力差距。

Result: 在文本主导数据集（MultiHuSE、UR-FUNNY）和音视频主导数据集（MUStARD）上评估。路径隔离显示，在所有测试配置下IAF均将主导模态内部精度保持在其单模态上限；对称融合在MultiHuSE上会使主导模态最多下降18.5%。IAF相比最强单模态基线最高提升8.25%。

Conclusion: 强模态坍缩是多模态融合低于单模态基线的关键机制；IAF通过保护主导模态并单向强化次模态来缓解该问题，表明融合设计应尊重模态间的不对等层级关系。

Abstract: Fusing multiple modalities is expected to improve model performance. However, on the MultiHuSE dataset, early, late, and symmetric attention fusion often fail to outperform the best unimodal baseline (text). Pathway isolation of a symmetric attention fusion model reveals that the text-pathway accuracy drops from 74.9% to 56.4% after fusion in one such setting, indicating that the dominant modality can be degraded during integration. We term this strong-modality collapse and argue that it helps explain why some multimodal models fail to surpass unimodal baselines. We propose Inverted Asymmetric Fusion (IAF), which avoids forcing mutual attention across modalities. The dominant modality is preserved by passing through fusion unchanged, while weaker modalities attend to it as a contextual anchor. Before fusion, weaker modalities are strengthened using Modality-Aware Knowledge Distillation. We evaluate IAF on three benchmarks with different modality hierarchies: text-dominant datasets (MultiHuSE, UR-FUNNY) and an audio-visual-dominant dataset (MUStARD). Pathway isolation shows that IAF preserves the dominant modality's internal accuracy at its unimodal ceiling across all tested configurations, whereas symmetric fusion degrades it by up to 18.5% on MultiHuSE. IAF improves over the strongest unimodal baseline by up to 8.25%.

</details>


### [66] [Pruning Binarized Neural Networks: A Dedicated Framework and Globally Weighted Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26233)
*Roan Rubiales,Jean Pierre David*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于 PyTorch 的研究型框架，用于设计、优化和评估二值化神经网络，该框架整合了冻结（freezing）与剪枝（pruning）机制。作者在该框架基础上提出了一种新的全局加权剪枝方法，该方法考虑参数在不同抽象层级上的相对重要性，从而在剪枝率与模型精度之间取得更优的权衡：在 VGG11 上以 70% 的剪枝率保持精度不变，而现有二值化设置下的最优方法仅能达到 41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的极端压缩（直至全二值化）能大幅降低内存占用和算术复杂度，便于在 FPGA、微控制器等受限边缘硬件上部署。将二值化与剪枝结合有望进一步带来效率提升，但现有剪枝策略并不适用于二值化表示，且很少能转化为实际的硬件收益。因此需要专门针对二值化神经网络设计的剪枝方案与评测框架。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个基于 PyTorch 的研究型框架，集成冻结与剪枝机制，支持对现有先进方法的快速、可复现评估以及新方法的快速原型开发；随后在此框架上提出一种新的剪枝方法，该方法通过跨抽象层级（abstraction levels）衡量学习参数的相对重要性，形成全局性的权重加权机制。

Result: 所提出的全局加权剪枝方法在模型精度与剪枝率之间始终取得更优权衡：在 VGG11 上实现 70% 剪枝率且精度保持不变，而二值化设定下的现有最优方法仅能达到 41% 的剪枝率。

Conclusion: 该框架为二值化神经网络的研究提供了可复现的评测与快速原型平台，且所提出的跨层级全局加权剪枝策略显著优于现有方法，为极低比特量化网络的高效压缩与硬件部署开辟了有效路径。

Abstract: Extreme compression of deep neural networks, up to full binarization, dramatically reduces memory footprint and arithmetic complexity, facilitating deployment on constrained edge hardware with field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs) and microcontrollers. Although combining binarization with pruning promises additional efficiency gains, existing pruning strategies are ill-suited to binarized representations and rarely translate into meaningful hardware savings. We introduce a PyTorch-based, research-oriented framework that incorporates freezing and pruning mechanisms for designing and optimizing binarized neural networks. The framework enables rapid and reproducible evaluation of state-of-the-art approaches and the fast prototyping of new ones. Leveraging this framework, we propose a novel pruning method that accounts for the relative importance of learned parameters across abstraction levels. Such a global weighting mechanism consistently achieves a superior trade-off between model accuracy and pruning rate, achieving a 70% pruning rate on VGG11 with constant accuracy, while state-of-the-art results reach only 41% in the binarized setting.

</details>


### [67] [Muon with Finite Newton-Schulz: The Smoothing Benefit in Nonsmooth Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26288)
*Mingyi Li,Taira Tsuchiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上重新审视Muon优化器，证明其在大型语言模型预训练中使用的有限深度Newton-Schulz迭代不仅不是近似误差，反而对非光滑非凸优化有益。通过online-to-nonconvex转换，作者表明有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极坐标映射光滑化为奇值的Lipschitz映射，从而使Muon成为带光滑谱势的在线学习器。只需对数级的Newton-Schulz深度即可保证收敛到驻点，而使用精确极坐标更新的Muon反而可能不收敛。所得样本复杂度界与非光滑非凸优化最优已知界匹配，在光滑非凸情形下达到问题相关因子意义下的最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在LLM预训练中对矩阵参数展现出强优化性能，其核心是对动量做Newton-Schulz迭代以近似正交化。但现有理论要么将迭代替换为其逼近的精确极分解，要么把有限深度视为近似误差——即Muon实际运行的迭代在理论上只会破坏保证。这与实践经验矛盾，作者旨在破除这一理论盲区，证明有限深度Newton-Schulz在非光滑非凸优化中具有真实的、正面的理论价值。

Method: 采用online-to-nonconvex转换框架：将Muon的更新规则视为在线学习器，其遗憾界可转换为驻点平稳性保证。关键洞察是有限Newton-Schulz迭代将不连续的极映射（精确极分解）光滑化为奇值的Lipschitz映射，使Muon成为带光滑谱势的在线学习器。作者证明了为达到目标精度只需对数增长的Newton-Schulz深度即可保证收敛，并将论证推广到具有相同光滑性质的一般谱映射。

Result: 证明：（1）Newton-Schulz深度随目标精度对数增长即足以保证非光滑非凸优化收敛到驻点，而精确极坐标更新的Muon可能不收敛；（2）得到的样本复杂度界与非光滑非凸优化最优已知界匹配；（3）在光滑非凸优化中达到问题相关因子意义下的最优；（4）结论可延拓至Newton-Schulz之外的一般光滑谱映射族。

Conclusion: 有限Newton-Schulz迭代非但不是Muon的理论弱点，反而是其成功的关键机制：它对不连续极映射的光滑化作用恰好满足了online-to-nonconvex转换的要求，实现了可证明的收敛保证。该工作弥合了Muon实践与理论之间的鸿沟，并为设计具有光滑谱势的优化器提供了更一般的设计原则。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a strong optimizer for the matrix-valued parameters in large language model pretraining, approximately orthogonalizing its momentum with a few Newton-Schulz iterations. Existing theory either replaces this iteration with the exact polar factor it approximates, or treats its finite depth as an approximation error, and thus the iteration Muon actually runs can only hurt the guarantees. We show that finite Newton-Schulz can instead be beneficial for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. To this end, we analyze Muon through the online-to-nonconvex conversion, which views the update rule as an online learner and converts its regret bound into a stationarity guarantee. The finite Newton-Schulz iteration smooths the discontinuous polar map into a Lipschitz map of the singular values, and Muon with finite Newton-Schulz can be regarded as an online learner with a smoothed spectral potential. This smoothing is exactly what the conversion needs: we prove that a Newton-Schulz depth growing only logarithmically in the target accuracy suffices for convergence to stationary points in nonsmooth nonconvex optimization, whereas Muon with the exact-polar update may fail to converge. The resulting sample complexity bounds match the best-known guarantees for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and are optimal for smooth nonconvex optimization up to problem-dependent factors. The argument extends beyond Newton-Schulz to general spectral maps with the same smoothing property.

</details>


### [68] [Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26309)
*Antonia van Betteray,Jonathan Klees,Miriam Schäfers,Matthias Rottmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMELS（代数多重网格加速的高效标签传播框架），通过快速近邻图构建和代数多重网格求解器替代传统随机游走迭代，实现大规模高维数据集上的高效半监督标签传播。该方法能在单个多重网格循环中将标签信息传播到任意大小的图，相比现有实现显著降低运行时间，对超参数选择更鲁棒，在小样本标注条件下也能产生准确标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型依赖大量标注数据，但大规模数据集的人工标注昂贵且耗时。标签传播作为一种半监督学习方法可在少量标注样本与大量未标注数据间传播信息，但该方法在应用于大规模、高维数据集时受限于计算成本和内存约束。

Method: AMELS框架包含两大核心改进：（1）快速近邻图构建，降低图构建成本；（2）引入代数多重网格（AMG）求解器，以多层次的迭代求解方式替代标签传播中常用的随机游走迭代。由于AMG的多层特性，AMELS能够在一个多重网格循环内将标签信息传播到任意规模的图上。

Result: 实验表明，与现有实现相比，AMELS在运行时间上取得显著缩减，并且在运行时间和分类精度两方面对超参数选择均更鲁棒。该框架在大规模图像数据集上实现了高效标签传播，即使在仅有少量标注样本时也能产生准确的标签。

Conclusion: AMELS通过代数多重网格加速解决了标签传播在大规模高维数据上的可扩展性瓶颈，兼顾效率与鲁棒性，为有限标注条件下的图像分类等半监督学习任务提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models rely on large amounts of labeled data. However, manual annotation of large-scale datasets is expensive and time-consuming. Label spreading is a semi-supervised learning technique that addresses this challenge by propagating information from a few labeled examples to a larger pool of unlabeled data. Despite its effectiveness, its application to large-scale, high-dimensional datasets is limited by computational costs and memory constraints. To address these limitations, we propose Algebraic Multigrid Acceleration for Efficient Label Spreading (AMELS), an efficient label spreading framework that improves scalability by fast construction of neighborhood graphs and the incorporation of algebraic multigrid solvers. The latter is an iterative solver that replaces the ordinary random walk iteration typically performed in label spreading. Due to the multilevel nature of algebraic multigrid solvers, AMELS spreads given label information across a graph of any size in a single multigrid cycle. We demonstrate that AMELS achieves significant runtime reductions compared to existing implementations while also being more robust to hyperparameter choices in terms of both runtime and classification accuracy. Our framework therefore enables efficient label spreading on large-scale image datasets and produces accurate labels even when only a few labeled samples are available.

</details>


### [69] [Privacy Without Regret: Differentially Private Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26324)
*Ishi Jain,Nandini Bhattad,Sayak Ray Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对推理时对齐中最常用的 Best-of-N（BoN）采样所面临的两个缺陷——奖励黑客攻击与敏感人类偏好数据的隐私泄露——提出单一干预方案：在奖励分数上添加校准噪声。作者提出 PrivBoN（Gumbel 噪声），证明其可同时实现 ε-差分隐私与 KL 正则化对齐，且在隐私预算超过临界阈值 ε* 时，隐私驱动的噪声恰为遗憾最优正则化，对齐零额外成本；又提出 PrivITP（χ² 正则化拒绝采样 + 两阶段高斯机制），获得与响应数 n 无关的逐事后 (ε,δ)-DP，将正则化与隐私参数解耦，并达到信息论天花板（至多含噪声膨胀项）。实验证明两种方法具有缩放单调性，且 PrivITP 在同等隐私水平下优于或持平 PrivBoN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoN 是部署最广泛的推理时对齐策略，但存在两个互不相关的问题：一是代理奖励模型误差导致的奖励黑客（所选响应利用奖励模型的缺陷）；二是用于训练奖励模型的人类偏好数据缺乏隐私保护。

Method: 提出“在奖励评分前添加校准噪声”这一统一干预。PrivBoN：在选样前向奖励分数注入适当尺度的 Gumbel 噪声，同时提供 ε-差分隐私并实现 KL 正则化对齐。PrivITP：将 χ² 正则化的拒绝采样与两阶段高斯机制结合，实现逐事后 (ε,δ)-差分隐私，其隐私成本与候选响应数 n 无关，并实现正则化参数与隐私参数的干净解耦。

Result: 理论结果表明，当隐私预算超过临界阈值 ε* 时，隐私要求的噪声本身就是遗憾最优正则化，隐私不造成额外对齐成本，可匹配 Huang et al. (2025) 的信息论天花板；PrivITP 亦能接近该天花板（相差一个噪声膨胀项）。实验在多种语言模型、数据集与奖励模型上验证：PrivBoN 与 PrivITP 均呈缩放单调性（而 BoN 在超过临界 n 后性能退化），且在同等隐私水平下 PrivITP 匹配或优于 PrivBoN，在强隐私区增益最大。

Conclusion: 单一干预（校准噪声）可同时解决 BoN 的奖励黑客与隐私保护问题；PrivBoN 与 PrivITP 在理论最优性与实践经验上均优于原始 BoN，为推理时对齐提供了兼顾隐私与鲁棒性的可行方案。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) sampling is the simplest and most widely deployed inference-time alignment strategy, but it suffers from two distinct problems: reward hacking, in which the selected response exploits errors in the proxy reward model, and the absence of any privacy protection for the sensitive human preference data used to train that reward model. We show that a single intervention-adding calibrated noise to reward scores before selection-resolves both. Our first result, Private Best-of-N (PrivBoN), establishes that Gumbel noise at an appropriate scale simultaneously provides $ε$-differential privacy and implements KL-regularized alignment. Whenever the privacy budget exceeds a critical threshold $ε^*$, the privacy-mandated noise is the regret-optimal regularization, and privacy imposes zero additional alignment cost-matching the information-theoretic skyline of Huang et al. (2025). Because $ε^*$ depends on an unknown coverage coefficient, we introduce Private Inference-Time Pessimism (PrivITP), which combines $χ^2$-regularized rejection sampling with a two-phase Gaussian mechanism. PrivITP achieves ex-post $(ε,δ)$-DP with a privacy cost independent of the number of responses $n$, cleanly decouples the regularization parameter from the privacy parameter, and attains the skyline up to a noise-inflation term. Experiments across several language models, datasets, and reward models confirm our results: PrivBoN and PrivITP are scaling-monotonic (unlike BoN, which degrades past a critical $n$), and PrivITP matches or outperforms PrivBoN at equivalent privacy levels, with the largest gains in the strong-privacy regime.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond Capability Benchmarks: Learning Operational Fingerprints of LLM Cloud Services from Production Incident Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26332)
*Meiwei Zhang,Eduardo Miranda,Bruce Baynes,Suvigya Jain,Wanlong Chen,Tao He,Sergey Borodavkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OpEmbed，一个从结构化、隐私保护的工单元数据中为 LLM 云服务学习紧凑“操作指纹”（operational fingerprint）的框架。它不依赖工单文本，而是将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名，并通过时间对比学习、跨视图重建和世代序性正则化学习低维表示。在 Google Cloud 超过 33,000 个生产工单（涵盖 7 个 LLM 家族、26 个月）上的评估表明，OpEmbed 能恢复可解释的家族级与版本级结构，提升留一模型预测性能，在早期窗口数据有限时依然有效，并支持跨模型故障类型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管 LLM 服务已进入真实生产系统，但模型选型和服务的规划仍主要依赖能力基准（capability benchmarks），这类基准无法反映部署后的运行行为；同时直接使用工单文本会带来隐私和文本质量问题，因此需要一种基于结构化、隐私保护的元数据来刻画云服务运维行为的方法。

Method: OpEmbed 将模型-时间窗口聚合为八通道操作签名，然后通过三类自监督/正则化损失学习低维表示：时间对比学习（temporal contrastive learning）、跨视图重建（cross-view reconstruction）以及世代序性正则化（generational-ordinality regularization），全程不使用工单文本内容。

Result: 在 Google Cloud 超过 33,000 个生产工单、7 个 LLM 家族、26 个月的数据上，OpEmbed 恢复了可解释的家族级与版本级结构；在留一模型（leave-one-model-out）操作预测任务上超过非学习基线；在早期窗口数据有限时仍保持有用；并支持跨模型的故障类型迁移。

Conclusion: 作者报告了在模型上线（onboarding）、支持就绪评估（support readiness assessment）和运行监控（operational monitoring）等场景中构建和评估该工具的实践教训，表明基于工单元数据的学习式操作指纹可作为能力基准之外的重要补充，用于生产环境的 LLM 服务规划与运维。

Abstract: Managed LLM services are now part of real production systems, but model selection and service planning still rely heavily on capability benchmarks that reveal little about operational behavior after deployment. We present Operational Embedding (OpEmbed), a framework for learning compact operational fingerprints of LLM cloud services from structured, privacy-preserving support-case metadata, without using case text. OpEmbed aggregates model--time windows into an eight-channel operational signature and learns a low-dimensional representation via temporal contrastive learning, cross-view reconstruction, and generational-ordinality regularization. Evaluated on more than 33,000 production support cases spanning seven LLM families over 26 months at Google Cloud, OpEmbed recovers interpretable family- and version-level structure, improves leave-one-model-out operational forecasting over non-learned baselines, remains useful under limited early-window data, and supports cross-model fault-type transfer. We report the practical lessons learned from building and evaluating this tool for model onboarding, support readiness assessment, and operational monitoring.

</details>


### [71] [CG4AI: A Column Generation Framework for Training AI Models Under Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26375)
*Youcef Magnouche,Abderrahmane Driouch,Sébastien Martin,Pierre Bauguion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CG4AI 框架，通过对多个 AI 模型进行凸组合并在组合输出上施加线性约束，从而在训练损失最小化之外提供可验证的可行性保证。主线性规划（LP）确定最优混合权重，定价子问题利用对偶变量生成新模型以聚焦最被违反的约束，切割平面过程将可行性保证扩展到训练集之外。在 MNIST 数字分类与多商品流问题上的实验表明，CG4AI 能可靠地产生满足约束的预测器，且精度优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习训练只最小化数据集上的损失函数，无法保证模型输出满足预定义的规则或约束。然而在自动驾驶、网络路由等许多现实应用中，这种可行性保证至关重要，因此需要一种能够在模型训练过程中显式融入并强制满足约束的框架。

Method: CG4AI 构建多个 AI 模型的凸组合，并在组合输出上强制执行线性约束。其核心由三部分组成：主线性规划（LP）用于确定最优混合权重；定价子问题根据 LP 对偶变量生成新模型，聚焦于被违反最严重的约束；切割平面（cutting-plane）过程把可行性保证从训练集推广到更广的输入空间。

Result: 在 MNIST 上演示了四种约束用法：仅从约束学习、提升对抗鲁棒性、修正错误分类样本以及强制输出重标记；在多商品流问题中，对神经网络路由预测器强制执行链路容量约束。在 MNIST 和标准 SNDLIB 基准网络上的实验表明，CG4AI 能稳定地生成满足约束的预测器，同时精度优于单模型基线。

Conclusion: CG4AI 提供了一种通用且有效的机制，将线性输出约束系统地集成进 AI 模型组合框架中，兼顾可行性与精度，并展示了其在监督学习（分类）和结构化预测（路由优化）两类问题上的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Standard machine-learning training minimizes a loss function over a dataset, but does not guarantee that the resulting model will satisfy predefined rules or constraints on its outputs. In many real-world applications, ranging from autonomous systems to network routing, such guarantees are essential. We propose CG4AI, a framework that builds a convex combination of AI models while enforcing linear constraints on the combined output. A master linear program (LP) determines the optimal mixture weights, while a pricing subproblem generates new models guided by LP dual variables, focusing attention on the most violated constraints. A cutting-plane procedure extends feasibility guarantees beyond the training set. We apply CG4AI to two problems: (i) digit classification on MNIST, where we demonstrate four distinct uses of constraints, learning from constraints alone, improving adversarial robustness, correcting misclassified examples, and enforcing output relabeling; and (ii) the multi-commodity flow problem, where link capacity constraints are enforced on neural-network routing predictors. Experiments on MNIST and standard SNDLIB benchmark networks show that CG4AI reliably produces feasible predictors while achieving better accuracy than single-model baselines.

</details>


### [72] [The Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy: A Framework for Constructing Classifiers and Diagnosing Their Decisions, Applied to Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26423)
*Jaturong Kongmanee,Smile Thanapattheerakul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为 Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy 的框架，用于构建作为安全防护层的分类器，并配套开发诊断工具来判定分类器的“自信决策”中有哪些可被信任。该框架由三步组成：通过交叉验证选取嵌入维度的分类器、定位约 29% 训练样本构成的潜在支持向量机集合以识别能改变预测标签的关键 token，以及利用这些 token 及其攻击幅度构建诊断分类体系。该分类体系将提示词划分到不同处理区域：可安全依赖（Rely Safely）、启发式偏差/覆盖（Heuristic Bias/Override）、以及需要转交人工/安全审查的信息不足（Insufficient Context）。在公开的提示注入数据集上，约 77% 的自信决策在删除单个 token 后不再稳健，这种脆弱性可分解为两类失败模式：置信度校准失效与真正可被利用的捷径；文中还为每个区域给出了修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分类器（尤其是安全防护层面的分类器）常给出高置信度预测，但高置信度并不等于可靠，模型可能因训练捷径或校准缺陷而犯错。因此需要一种系统化的方式来判断哪些自信决策可以信赖、哪些需要人工审查，从而兼顾自动化效率与安全性。

Method: 框架包含三部分：(i) 构建维度优化的分类器，嵌入维度由交叉验证性能经验选取而非预先固定；(ii) 定位一小批潜在支持向量（约占训练样本 29%），代表有影响力的提示词，用于识别会改变分类器预测标签的 token；(iii) 利用这些 token 及其攻击幅度构建诊断分类体系（Taxonomy），将提示词划分为 Rely Safely、Heuristic Bias、Heuristic Override、Insufficient Context 等处理区域，并针对每个区域给出修复策略。

Result: 在公开的提示注入数据集上，约 77% 的自信决策在删除单个 token 后不再稳健，表明模型存在系统性脆弱性。这种脆弱性可分离为两类模式：置信度校准失败和真正可被利用的捷径，表明同一自信现象背后有不同的失败机理，需要不同的处理方式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种端到端的诊断指南：对可安全依赖的决策直接采纳，将启发式偏差与覆盖标记出来，将信息不足的案例转交人工/安全审查，从而在自动化防护与人工监督之间形成明确的分流策略；作者以一系列步骤演示了框架每一阶段如何运作。

Abstract: This paper proposes a framework for constructing a classifier as a safeguard layer, and for developing a complementary diagnostic that identifies which of the classifier's confident decisions can be trusted. This framework, the Latent Diagnostic Taxonomy, consists of (i) constructing a dimensionality-optimized classifier, in which the embedding dimensionality is empirically selected via cross-validated performance rather than fixed a priori, (ii) locating a relatively small set of latent support vectors (~ 29% of total training examples) representing influential prompts for identifying tokens that alter the classifier's predicted labels, and (iii) utilizing such tokens and their associated attack magnitudes for constructing a diagnostic taxonomy. This diagnostic taxonomy provides an end-to-end guideline for flagging prompts that require different treatments: rely Safely on the classifier's decision; flag Heuristic Bias and Heuristic Override cases; route Insufficient Context cases for further human/safety review. Applying the framework to a classifier trained on a public prompt injection dataset, we find that a substantial fraction of its confident decisions (~ 77%) are not robust to removing a single token, and that this brittleness separates into two distinct failure patterns: a confidence calibration failure and a genuinely exploitable shortcut. For each zone of the taxonomy, we also recommend strategies for remediating diagnosed prompts. We illustrate the framework as a series of steps, demonstrating how each step operates.

</details>


### [73] [FedCMAPSS: A Benchmark for Federated Learning in Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26433)
*Amelia Sorrenti,Matteo Pennisi,Concetto Spampinato,Simone Palazzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FedCMAPSS——一个基于 NASA C-MAPSS 数据集的联邦剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计基准。该基准定义了五种标准任务以模拟从理想 IID 到极端统计异构等真实工业场景，并系统评估了多种先进联邦优化算法在不同神经网络架构上的表现。通过公开源码与数据划分，为联邦预测性维护提供可复现的基础平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的预测性健康管理（PHM）是工业 4.0 的关键使能技术，但 RUL 估计模型的开发常受限于运行至失效（run-to-failure）数据的稀缺。联邦学习可在不共享传感器数据的前提下协同训练预测模型，具有很大潜力，然而此前研究缺乏统一的评估框架，难以公平比较和复现结果。为填补这一空白，作者构建了 FedCMAPSS 基准。

Method: 基于常用的 NASA C-MAPSS 数据集构建联邦 RUL 估计基准，设计五类标准任务以模拟真实工业挑战，覆盖从理想的独立同分布（IID）设置到极端统计异构（non-IID）场景；并在多种神经网络架构上对当前最先进的联邦优化算法进行系统性、标准化的评估，同时公开源代码与数据划分以保证可复现性。

Result: 建立了一套可复现的基线与标准化评估流程，对先进联邦优化算法在多种网络架构上的性能进行了系统比较，并公开了源码与数据划分，为后续研究提供统一的对比基准。

Conclusion: FedCMAPSS 旨在为联邦预测性维护解决方案的开发和比较提供标准基础，推动该领域形成统一的评估范式，从而促进数据稀缺条件下 RUL 估计模型的稳健发展。

Abstract: Data-driven prognostics and health management has emerged as a key enabler for Industry 4.0, yet the development of robust remaining useful life (RUL) estimation models is often limited by the scarcity of run-to-failure data. While federated learning offers a promising paradigm to collaboratively train predictive models without sharing sensor data, research efforts have operated so far in the absence of a common evaluation framework. To address this gap, this paper introduces FedCMAPSS, a benchmark for federated RUL estimation based on the commonly-used NASA C-MAPSS dataset.
