We define a set of five standardized tasks designed to simulate real-world industrial challenges, ranging from ideal IID settings to extreme statistical heterogeneity, and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art federated optimization algorithms across multiple neural architectures. By establishing reproducible baselines and making the source code and data splits publicly available, this work aims to provide a standard foundation for developing and comparing federated predictive maintenance solutions.

</details>


### [74] [NeoTriFuse: Reliability-Aware Multimodal Fusion under Missingness Heterogeneity for Neonatal Mortality Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26436)
*Jiyuan Tian,Qincheng Shen,Ye Lin,Yu Gao,Haohui Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeoTriFuse，一种面向新生儿监测数据的可靠性感知多模态融合框架，将缺失率建模为显式可靠性信号并动态调节模态贡献，整合静态围产期变量、局部-全局时序编码器与患者级统计摘要，联合优化死亡预测与辅助住院时长目标，取得F1 0.6736±0.0216、AUROC 0.9454±0.0056的性能，消融与敏感性分析表明框架在临床缺失条件下稳健有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿死亡风险预测面临严重类别不平衡、临床风险因素异质、多尺度时间动态与大量数据缺失等挑战；传统多模态方法仅将缺失视为预处理问题，未利用缺失本身携带的可靠性信息，难以在真实临床缺失条件下取得稳健预测。

Method: NeoTriFuse采用可靠性感知多模态融合框架：1) 将缺失显式建模为可靠性信号，通过可靠性引导的门控机制动态调节各模态在融合中的贡献；2) 融合三类模态——静态围产期变量、局部-全局时序编码器（捕捉多尺度时间动态）、患者级统计摘要；3) 联合优化死亡预测主任务与住院时长辅助任务。

Result: NeoTriFuse取得F1分数0.6736±0.0216、AUROC 0.9454±0.0056。消融实验表明局部-全局时序架构与患者级摘要分支贡献最大，可靠性感知门控在异质观测完整性下进一步改善阈值依赖指标；敏感性分析显示相邻超参数设置下性能稳定。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在现实临床缺失条件下，可靠性感知多模态融合是新生儿死亡预测的实用方案，将缺失作为信息信号而非仅待处理的问题，可提升预测的稳健性与实用性。

Abstract: Neonatal mortality risk prediction from bedside monitoring data remains challenging due to extreme class imbalance, heterogeneous clinical risk factors, multi-scale temporal dynamics, and substantial missingness. We propose NeoTriFuse, a reliability-aware multimodal fusion framework for missingness-heterogeneous neonatal monitoring data. Unlike conventional multimodal approaches that treat missingness primarily as a preprocessing issue, NeoTriFuse models missingness as an explicit reliability signal that dynamically modulates modality contributions during fusion. The framework integrates static perinatal variables, local-global temporal encoders, and patient-level statistical summaries through reliability-guided gating mechanisms, while jointly optimizing mortality prediction and an auxiliary length-of-stay objective. NeoTriFuse achieves competitive performance, with an F1 score of 0.6736 +/- 0.0216 and an AUROC of 0.9454 +/- 0.0056. Ablation studies indicate that the local-global temporal architecture and patient-level summary branch contribute most substantially to predictive performance, while reliability-aware gating provides additional improvements on threshold-dependent metrics under heterogeneous observation completeness. Sensitivity analyses further suggest stable performance across nearby hyperparameter settings. Overall, the findings support reliability-aware multimodal fusion as a practical approach for neonatal mortality prediction under realistic clinical missingness conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26437)
*Haohui Lu,jiyuan Tian,Fangyu Zhou,Shahadat Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图神经网络中由敏感同质性与消息传递引起的结构性偏差，本文提出SF-GNN（子图过滤公平图神经网络）：一种轻量级、与架构无关的框架，通过识别并随机构边缘过滤（结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器，如中心节点参与和三元闭包）在聚合阶段从源头缓解偏差，并配合统计奇偶性正则化与预热调度，在五个基准数据集上取得更优的公平性-准确性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使敏感属性被排除在节点特征之外，GNN仍可能因图拓扑与消息传递在敏感同质性下传播群体相关信号而产生不公平行为。现有公平感知GNN方法大多在全局层面对表示或预测分布施加约束，未显式控制偏倚信息在聚合过程中传播的局部结构路径。

Method: SF-GNN在源头缓解结构性偏差：首先通过结合敏感同质性与结构传播放大器（中心节点参与、三元闭包）识别偏差易发边；然后在每次消息传递步骤中引入随机边缘过滤，选择性降权或移除这些边，同时保留其余图结构；训练中加入带预热调度的统计奇偶性正则化以稳定优化。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，SF-GNN在保持竞争性预测性能的同时实现一致的公平性提升，相比近期公平感知GNN基线获得更好的公平性-准确性权衡。

Conclusion: SF-GNN证明通过显式干预传播路径中的局部结构（边缘过滤）而非仅做全局约束，能更有效地实现公平且保持性能；其轻量、架构无关的特性使其成为实用的公平GNN框架。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) can exhibit unfair behavior even when sensitive attributes are excluded from node features, because graph topology and message passing propagate group-correlated signals under sensitive homophily. Existing fairness-aware GNN methods mainly constrain representations or prediction distributions at a global level, without explicitly controlling the local structural pathways through which biased information propagates during aggregation. We propose Subgraph Filtering for Fair Graph Neural Networks (SF-GNN), a lightweight and architecture-agnostic framework that mitigates structural bias at its source. SF-GNN identifies bias-prone edges by combining sensitive homophily with structural propagation amplifiers, including hub participation and triadic closure. It then incorporates stochastic edge filtering into each message-passing step to selectively downweight or remove these edges while preserving the remaining graph structure. Training further incorporates a statistical-parity regularizer with a warm-up schedule to stabilize optimization. Experiments on five benchmark datasets show that SF-GNN achieves consistent fairness improvements while maintaining competitive predictive performance, leading to a better fairness--accuracy trade-off than recent fairness-aware GNN baselines.

</details>


### [76] [Toward Equitable Low-Carbon Mobility: Fairness-Aware Demand Prediction for Expanding Bike-Sharing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26451)
*Man Luo,Yixuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairGIN 是一个面向扩张中共享单车系统的公平感知图神经网络，通过扩张模拟增量训练、基于注意力的知识迁移和公平感知优化三个组件，在提升冷启动预测精度的同时减少基于收入的社会不平等，且不牺牲整体系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享单车系统持续扩张带来两大挑战：新部署站点缺乏历史骑行记录，导致图模型在演化网络中训练与推理分布不匹配（冷启动问题）；历史需求数据可能编码结构性不平等——低收入社区的低骑行量反映的是基础设施接入受限而非潜在需求弱，直接在这些数据上训练会强化现有的出行差距。

Method: FairGIN 集成三个组件：(1) Expansion-Simulated Increment Training（扩张模拟增量训练），在训练时随机模拟网络扩张以缩小冷启动分布差距；(2) Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer（基于注意力的知识迁移），结合站点自适应温度缩放与正交嵌入对齐，将表示从数据丰富的既有站点迁移到数据稀疏的新站点；(3) Fairness-Aware Optimization（公平感知优化），引入按收入分层的正则化与公平校准的部署评分，支持更具包容性的站点布局。

Result: 在纽约和西雅图数据集上的实验表明，FairGIN 在各种扩张场景下均达到最先进的预测精度，同时显著降低了基于收入的差距，且不损害整体系统效率。

Conclusion: FairGIN 证明可以通过结合冷启动缓解与公平感知机制，使共享单车需求预测模型在演化网络中兼顾预测准确性与社会公平，为城市出行系统的公平可持续扩张提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bike-sharing systems are an important component of low-carbon urban mobility, but continued expansion creates challenges in both cold-start prediction and equitable resource allocation. Newly deployed stations lack historical ridership records, causing a mismatch between training and inference for graph-based models on evolving networks. Historical demand may also encode structural inequalities, as lower ridership in low-income neighborhoods can reflect limited infrastructure access rather than weak latent demand. Models trained directly on such data may therefore reinforce existing mobility disparities. We propose FairGIN, a fairness-aware graph neural network for demand prediction in expanding bike-sharing systems. FairGIN integrates three components. Expansion-Simulated Increment Training stochastically simulates network expansion during training to reduce the cold-start distribution gap. Attention-Based Knowledge Transfer combines station-adaptive temperature scaling with orthogonal embedding alignment to transfer representations from data-rich existing stations to data-sparse new stations. Fairness-Aware Optimization introduces income-stratified regularization and an equity-calibrated deployment score to support more inclusive station placement. Experiments on NYC and Seattle demonstrate that FairGIN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy across diverse expansion scenarios while substantially reducing income-based disparities without compromising overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [77] [Distributed Training using an Intelligent Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26453)
*Nihar Shah,Ben Blier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出让广域网（WAN）成为训练过程的主动参与者：系统层面利用组播技术复制出站流量、利用在线FPGA聚合入站流量，以缓解出口/入口瓶颈；算法层面提出一个优化框架，围绕网络拓扑和上述技术生成丰富的同步调度方案（即“旋转的岛屿团（clique）”），从而最大化信息交换。作者在以DoubleZero网络为模型的九城市拓扑上进行了验证，结果表明最优调度会随网络能力而变化，并且这些技术能缩小与同地训练（gold standard）之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨广域网（WAN）的分布式训练极具挑战性：计算“岛屿”之间的持续参数交换受限于有限带宽、高延迟与不均匀的拓扑。作者希望改变传统“网络仅作为传输管道”的做法，让网络主动参与训练，以突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 两方面的技术方案：（1）系统侧：采用组播技术复制出站流量以缓解出口（egress）瓶颈，并采用在线FPGA聚合入站流量以缓解入口（ingress）瓶颈；这些技术此前用于数据中心内，本文将其扩展到WAN。（2）算法侧：开发一个优化框架，基于底层网络拓扑和上述技术构造丰富的同步调度方案（即围绕网络不断旋转的岛屿团/子集合），以最大化信息交换。

Result: 以基于DoubleZero网络（一种同时具备组播与在线FPGA的实时可编程WAN）的九城市拓扑为例进行验证，展示了最优调度如何随网络能力的变化而改变，并表明这些方法可缩小与同地训练这一黄金标准之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过将网络变为训练的主动参与者（系统上的组播+在线FPGA、算法上的同步调度优化），可以显著缓解WAN中有限带宽、高延迟与不均匀拓扑带来的约束，使跨WAN分布式训练逼近同地训练的性能。

Abstract: Distributed training across a wide area network (WAN) is challenging, as continuous parameter exchange by islands of compute is constrained by limited bandwidth, high latency, and uneven topology. We propose making the network an active participant in training. On the systems side, such networks should leverage (i) multicast technology to replicate outbound traffic and (ii) in-line FPGAs to aggregate inbound traffic, to ease egress and ingress bottlenecks. These technologies are used for training across workers within a data center, but this paper extends them to the WAN. On the algorithms side, we develop an optimization framework that produces rich synchronization schedules (namely, rotating cliques of islands) around the underlying network topology and these technologies, to maximize information exchange. Finally, we illustrate this on a nine-city topology modeled on the DoubleZero network, a live programmable WAN equipped with both technologies, and show how the optimal schedules shift with the network's capabilities. Together, these can narrow the gap to the gold standard of colocated training.

</details>


### [78] [Diff Mining: Logit Differences Reveal Finetuning Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26462)
*Greg Kocher,Robert West,Clément Dumas,Julian Minder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Diff Mining，一种仅通过对比微调模型与基座模型输出 logits 差来识别模型微调学习内容的简单有效框架。它能在与微调领域无关的文本上提取被放大的显著 token，作为微调训练的"指纹"，并在多项任务中显著优于现有 SOTA 模型对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为改进语言模型行为和引入新行为的黄金标准，但微调过程中究竟产生了哪些行为往往不明确。随着模型能力增强，理解微调过程愈发重要，尤其因为微调可能引入不良行为。现有模型对比方法多需要访问模型内部结构，缺乏仅基于输出的一种可扩展的审计工具。

Method: Diff Mining 框架包含两个模块化阶段：(i) 在参考语料上提取微调模型与基座模型之间逐上下文的 logit 差异；(ii) 聚合所得信号，构建可解释的 token 集合来表征微调内容。聚合阶段探索了两种方法：简单的 Top-K 频率法，以及基于非负矩阵分解（NMF）的方法，用于将多个微调目标解耦为不同的 token 簇。该框架仅需访问输出 logits，无需模型内部信息，可扩展到大型模型。

Result: 实验在多种场景下验证了 Diff Mining 的有效性：在微调领域检测任务上，它显著优于先进的模型对比方法，无论是在识别相关 token 方面，还是在将提取的 token 集合提供给可解释性智能体时的下游性能上；在注入偏见的模型上，它无需定向探测即可识别出超过三分之一的偏见。

Conclusion: Diff Mining 展示了作为审计工具检测微调目标的前景，通过仅输出 logits 即可有效识别微调模型学习到的内容，为开发监控和审计语言模型微调行为的工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Finetuning has become the gold standard for refining existing behaviors and inducing new ones in language models, yet it often remains unclear exactly which behaviors emerge during this process. As models grow ever more capable, understanding finetuning better becomes increasingly important, particularly since unwanted behaviors may arise during finetuning. In this paper, we introduce Diff Mining, a simple yet effective framework for identifying what a finetuned model has learned by comparing its logits to those of its base model. Diff Mining effectively surfaces salient tokens that are amplified in the finetuned model, serving as a fingerprint of its training -- even on text unrelated to the finetuning domain. Unlike many existing model diffing methods which require model internals, Diff Mining only needs access to output logits and scales to large models. The framework consists of two modular stages: (i) extracting per-context logit differences between the finetuned and base models on a reference corpus, and (ii) aggregating the resulting signals to construct an interpretable token set representing the finetune. For aggregation, we explore both a simple Top-K frequency method and a Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF)-based approach for disentangling multiple finetuning objectives into distinct token clusters. Empirically, Diff Mining succeeds across diverse settings: on finetune domain detection, it significantly outperforms state-of-the-art model diffing methods both in identifying relevant tokens and in downstream performance when an interpretability agent is given access to the extracted token set; on models with injected biases, it identifies more than one third of the biases without targeted probing. Overall, our framework shows promise in developing auditing tools to detect finetuning objectives.

</details>


### [79] [Active Curriculum Refinement for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26469)
*Zhenya Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH 是一个课程学习框架，在由前提关系（如难度递增的编辑或参数增量）构成的DAG课程图上进行主动学习：先通过采样多样课程路径扩大覆盖，再将训练资源重新分配到尚未掌握的区域，在多样环境中展现出强大的鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习环境之间存在前提关系（prerequisite relations），形成有向无环课程图（DAG）。虽然这类结构常被隐式利用，但显式建模该结构可以更有效地指导训练，因此作者提出显式利用课程图进行课程学习。

Method: PATH框架包含两个阶段：第一阶段通过采样多样化的课程路径（curriculum paths）扩大对课程图的覆盖；第二阶段将训练资源重新分配到图中尚未掌握（unmastered）的区域，实现主动学习式的资源再分配。

Result: 在多种不同环境上的实验表明，PATH显式利用课程图结构，取得了强鲁棒性与强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模并主动学习课程图结构能够有效提升强化学习的训练效果，PATH框架验证了该思路的可行性，在鲁棒性和泛化性方面均表现优异。

Abstract: In many reinforcement learning (RL) domains, environments are connected by prerequisite relations, such as difficulty-increasing edits or parameter increments, which induce a directed acyclic curriculum graph (DAG). Although this structure is often exploited only implicitly, explicitly modeling it can improve training. We introduce PATH, a curriculum-learning framework that performs active learning over the curriculum graph. PATH first expands coverage by sampling diverse curriculum paths and then reallocates training toward regions that remain unmastered. Experiments across diverse environments show that PATH explicitly leverages the graph structure to achieve strong robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [80] [Shared Actors Need Not Share Critics: Effects of Value Mismatch in Parallel Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26481)
*Zhenya Liu,Yang Meng,Zhuokai Zhao,Xuefeng Liu,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了并行强化学习（如程序化生成关卡、随机动力学、课程学习）中多个环境共享同一 Critic 导致的价值不匹配（value mismatch）问题。通过多环境 bandit 模型的理论分析，作者刻画了共享 Critic 因无法分辨各环境不同的价值目标而使优势估计系统性偏移、进而扭曲策略更新的机制；并提出了一个极简干预方案——仅向 Critic 提供记录的（logged）环境索引，使其能分离各环境的价值目标。CartPole、MuJoCo 的受控实验验证了价值偏移与性能差距的预测，BipedalWalker 和 Procgen 实验表明该干预带来更稳定的学习与更高回报（16 个 Procgen 游戏、600 个未见关卡上归一化回报平均提升 40.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并行 RL 中，多个环境（如程序化关卡、随机动力学）共享一个 Critic 的做法很常见，但不同环境对同一可见输入可能赋予不同的期望回报。共享 Critic 因缺少环境信息必须调和不同的价值目标，从而系统性地偏移采样优势，影响策略更新的质量。作者希望从理论上阐明这一被忽视的机制（仅用标量估计器方差无法刻画），并基于理论提出简单有效的修复方案。

Method: （1）理论分析：构建多个环境共享最优臂的 bandit 模型，刻画价值不匹配如何重分布采样策略更新——强化无益动作、削弱甚至反转有用动作；对比无基线、共享价值、环境特定价值三种 oracle 过程，证明其固定策略下的平均 logit 更新相同、收敛到同一最优策略，但实际学习路径差异显著。（2）干预方案：仅将记录的（logged）环境索引传入 Critic，形成多头条件 Critic（multihead conditional critic），使其分离各环境的价值目标。（3）实验验证：在 CartPole、MuJoCo 上进行受控实验，检验预测的价值偏移、优势偏移与性能差距；在更复杂的 BipedalWalker 和全部 16 个 Procgen 游戏上评估干预效果。

Result: 受控的 CartPole 与 MuJoCo 实验证实了理论预测的价值偏移、优势偏移和性能差距。在 BipedalWalker 与 Procgen 中，基于环境索引的条件干预带来更稳定的学习与更高的回报；在全部 16 个 Procgen 游戏中，多头条件 Critic 在每游戏 600 个未见关卡上使归一化聚合回报提升 40.8%。

Conclusion: 理论表明价值不匹配是共享 Critic 破坏随机学习动力学的一条直接机制，而这无法仅用标量估计器方差刻画；实验表明基于环境索引的 Critic 条件化在并行强化学习中普遍有效，是一个极简而实用的改进方向。

Abstract: When a single policy is trained in parallel across multiple environments of the same task, such as procedurally generated levels, randomized dynamics, or curricula, implementations commonly use one critic across all sampled environments. Yet different environments can assign different expected returns to the same input visible to the critic. A critic without environment information must then reconcile distinct value targets, systematically shifting the sampled advantages within individual environments. Using illustrative bandit models with multiple environments and a common optimal arm, we characterize how this value mismatch redistributes sampled policy updates, reinforcing unhelpful actions while attenuating or even reversing useful ones. The oracle processes using no baseline, the shared value, or the value specific to the sampled environment have the same mean logit update at a fixed policy and converge to the same optimal policy, yet their realized learning paths can differ sharply. The analysis motivates a minimal intervention: give only a logged environment index to the critic so that it can separate the value targets. Controlled CartPole and MuJoCo experiments expose the predicted shifted values, advantages, and performance gaps. In the more complex BipedalWalker and Procgen settings, the same intervention yields more stable learning and higher returns. Across all $16$ Procgen games, the multihead conditional critic improves aggregate normalized return on $600$ unseen levels per game by $40.8\%$. In conclusion, the theory identifies value mismatch as a direct mechanism through which critic sharing can degrade stochastic learning dynamics, not captured by scalar estimator variance alone, and the experiments show that conditioning on an index is broadly effective in parallel reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [81] [Bayesian methods and Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms for curve reconstruction and point cloud data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26490)
*Asir Intesar Tushar,Ioannis Sgouralis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全贝叶斯框架，用于表示点云数据并重建闭合曲线：将观测点建模为潜在位置的噪声扰动，这些位置被约束在由非参数先验正则化的底层曲线上，并通过一系列针对点云特征定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器进行后验推断。合成数据与真实LiDAR实验表明重建准确且能量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代成像与传感技术采集的点云数据虽能提供详细的几何描述，但常受数据量大、定位噪声和缺失信息困扰；此外，现有点云重建管线通常只返回单一最佳拟合结构，无法提供不确定性量化（UQ）。

Method: 方法分为三部分：1) 生成模型——观测点被建模为位于底层闭合曲线上潜在位置的噪声扰动，曲线由非参数先验正则化；2) 推断——采用一系列针对点云特征定制的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）采样器进行后验推断；3) 实验验证——在合成示例和真实LiDAR数据集上评估。

Result: 数值实验（包括合成示例和真实LiDAR数据集）表明，该方法能够实现准确的重建，并对恢复的曲线提供量化的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该全贝叶斯框架有效解决了点云重建中缺乏不确定性量化的问题，在准确重建闭合曲线的同时给出不确定度度量，适用于大规模、含噪和缺失的点云数据场景。

Abstract: Point-cloud data routinely captured by modern imaging and sensor technologies provide detailed geometric descriptions of objects and environments, but their analysis is hindered by large data volumes, localization noise, and missing information. In addition, existing point-cloud reconstruction pipelines typically return a single best-fit structure without uncertainty quantification. We introduce a fully Bayesian framework for representing point-cloud data and reconstructing closed curves, in which observed points are modeled as noisy perturbations of latent locations constrained to lie on the underlying curve that is regularized by a non-parametric prior. Posterior inference in our framework is carried out using a series of Markov chain Monte Carlo samplers tailored to point-cloud characteristics. Numerical experiments, including synthetic examples and real-world LiDAR datasets, show accurate reconstructions and quantified uncertainty over the recovered curves.

</details>


### [82] [A Unified Framework for Fair and Personalized Decentralized Learning under Communication Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26493)
*Krishnendu S. Tharakan,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对去中心化学习中的统计异质性、公平性与通信约束三大挑战，提出统一框架和算法DMFL-SQ：在图结构个性化模型训练中引入agnostic混合公平目标，并通过稀疏化、量化和事件触发同步降低通信开销。理论证明其在一般非凸目标下仍保持O(T^{-1/2})的Moreau-envelope平稳性收敛速率，并给出PAC-Bayes泛化界；CIFAR-10与MUSMET EEG实验表明该算法显著减少通信、保持预测性能并改善客户端间公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习虽提升可扩展性、隐私与鲁棒性，却加剧了客户端间统计异质性、公平性失衡和通信瓶颈三大根本性挑战。作者提出核心问题：在有限通信下，去中心化学习能达到何种公平程度？希望通过统一框架同时解决个性化、公平与压缩通信，而非牺牲其中之一。

Method: 提出DMFL-SQ算法，将基于通信图的个性化多任务学习与agnostic混合公平目标结合，并采用稀疏化、量化与事件触发同步来降低通信量。理论方面：推导一般非凸目标下期望平方Moreau-envelope平稳性的O(T^{-1/2})收敛保证，以及公平感知混合目标下的PAC-Bayes泛化界。

Result: 在CIFAR-10与真实异构MUSMET EEG数据集上的实验表明，DMFL-SQ在显著降低通信量的同时保持预测性能，并提升客户端间公平性。理论结果证明个性化、公平性和通信效率可在去中心化学习中联合实现，且不牺牲主导收敛速率。

Conclusion: 该研究证明在有限通信约束下，去中心化学习可以同时实现个性化、公平性与通信高效性，且保持主导的O(T^{-1/2})收敛速率，为去中心化系统在资源受限和异质数据场景下的公平训练提供了理论保障与实用算法。

Abstract: Decentralized learning systems aim to collaboratively train models across multiple clients without relying on a central coordinator. While decentralization improves scalability, privacy, and robustness, it also exacerbates three fundamental challenges: statistical heterogeneity across clients, fairness in client-level performance, and stringent communication constraints. This raises a natural question: \emph{how fair can decentralized learning be under limited communication?} We address this question by presenting a unified framework for decentralized learning under communication constraints, bringing together graph-based personalization, agnostic fairness, and compressed event-triggered communication. Specifically, we propose a new algorithm DMFL-SQ, a decentralized multi-task learning algorithm that couples personalized model training over a communication graph with an agnostic mixture fairness objective, while reducing communication through sparsification, quantization, and event-triggered synchronization. We establish convergence guarantees for general non-convex objectives and show that DMFL-SQ achieves an $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate in expected squared Moreau-envelope stationarity despite sparse, quantized, and event-triggered communication. We further derive PAC-Bayes generalization guarantees for the fairness-aware mixture objective. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and the real heterogeneous MUSMET EEG dataset demonstrate that DMFL-SQ substantially reduces communication while maintaining predictive performance and improving fairness across clients. Together, our theoretical and empirical results show that personalization, fairness, and communication efficiency can be jointly achieved in decentralized learning while preserving the dominant convergence rate.

</details>


### [83] [A Single Suffix to Break Them All: Basin-Aware Jailbreaks for Merged Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26506)
*Yu Zhe,Yixin Tan,Junhao Wei,Wang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个此前被忽视的模型合并安全风险：即使所有参与合并的模型都各自通过了安全对齐，合并后的模型仍可能暴露来自预训练基础模型的越狱漏洞。作者提出了一种新的威胁场景（攻击者无需访问合并系数或具体检查点，仅需共享预训练主干即可构造跨合并模型迁移的越狱提示），并设计 Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ) 方法，将越狱提示生成形式化为合并空间上的 min-max 优化，以产生跨合并模型家族可迁移的对抗后缀。实验表明 BAJ 在多种主干和合并设置下具有一致的高迁移成功率，且对现有防御依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对模型合并的安全影响理解不足，通常将合并风险归因于不安全的组成模型，并隐含假设"合并各自对齐的模型可保持安全性"。作者发现这一假设是错误的：即便所有组成模型都经过安全对齐，合并过程本身仍会暴露源自预训练基础模型的越狱风险。这一被忽视的漏洞驱动了新的威胁建模，即在仅共享预训练主干而无精确合并信息的情况下构造可迁移的越狱提示。

Method: 作者提出 Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)，将越狱提示生成建模为合并空间上的 min-max 优化问题：通过最大化目标损失来寻找在不同合并点（对应不同合并系数）上都有效的对抗后缀，从而获得跨合并模型家族的迁移性；同时利用损失景观（basin）结构来引导优化，使生成的扰动在合并模型的权重盆地中均有效。

Result: 在多种不同预训练主干和多种合并设置（不同类型的合并系数/合并方法）下，BAJ 生成的对抗后缀取得了一致且高的迁移攻击成功率；并且该攻击在现有的防御机制（如对抗性前缀检测、困惑度过滤等）下依然有效。

Conclusion: 模型合并会引入一个独立于组成模型安全状态的新安全漏洞——预训练基础模型固有的越狱倾向会在合并后被重新暴露。这意味着"合并安全对齐模型即安全"的假设不成立，安全社区需重新评估模型合并的威胁模型，并设计专门针对该类跨合并迁移攻击的防御措施。

Abstract: Model merging enables combining multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Prior work primarily attributes merging risks to unsafe constituent models, implicitly assuming that merging individually aligned models preserves safety. In contrast, we show that model merging reveals a previously overlooked jailbreak risk rooted in the pretrained foundation model, even when all constituent models are individually safety-aligned. Motivated by this observation, we study a new threat setting where an attacker constructs jailbreak prompts that generalize across merged models sharing the same pretrained backbone, without access to the exact merging coefficients or constituent checkpoints. To exploit this phenomenon, we propose \textbf{Basin-Aware Jailbreak (BAJ)}, which formulates jailbreak generation as a min--max optimization over the merging space to produce transferable adversarial suffixes across merged model families. Experiments across diverse backbones and merging settings show that BAJ achieves consistently high transfer success rates and remains effective under existing defenses.

</details>


### [84] [Algorithmic Principles For Multiclass Learning Are Hard To Come By: Limits of Regularization and Proper Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26516)
*Julian Asilis,Shaddin Dughmi,Vatsal Sharan,Alec Sun,Shang-Hua Teng,Chang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回答了统计学习理论中"学习是否可归约为适当学习（proper learning）"以及"适当/不适当学习能否由正则化器刻画"这两个问题，均给出否定答案，解决了先前工作中三个开放问题：存在不能嵌入任何适当可学习类别的可学习多类问题；刻画了适当学习所需训练误差的精确标度（每个适当可学习类都有渐进零误差的适当学习器，但任何次线性误差标度都是某些问题的必要）；并证明正则化不是通用学习器（存在不能用SRM或局部正则化器学习的类别）。同时给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并通过"显示偏好"的可积性刻画SRM可表示性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习理论的两个基本问题是"哪些预测问题可学习"和"如何学习"。可学习性已由组合维度优雅地刻画，但"如何学习"一直难以捉摸：通用多类学习器依赖指数大小的一包含结构的复杂定向，而适当学习和正则化这些熟悉的算法原则仍缺乏理解。作者受先前工作启发，追问学习能否归结为（在更大假设类上的）适当学习，以及适当/不适当学习能否由合适的正则化器捕获。

Method: 作者通过构造反例和精确刻画来回答上述问题：构造一个可学习但不能嵌入任何适当可学习类别的多类问题；推导适当学习所需训练误差的精确刻画（普遍存在o(m)误差的适当学习器，同时任何给定的次线性标度a_m=o(m)都会在某个适当可学习问题中成为必要）；构造不能由任何SRM学习器学习的适当可学习类，以及不能由任何局部正则化器学习的可学习类。在正面结果中，作者提出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件，并利用显示偏好的可积性刻画SRM可表示性。

Result: 主要结果是三个否定性定理：(1) 存在可学习的多类问题无法嵌入任何适当可学习类，表明不能通过扩大假设类把学习归约为适当学习；(2) 适当学习确实可能需要训练误差，且该现象被精确刻画——每个适当可学习类存在误差为o(m)的适当学习器，但每个给定的次线性标度a_m=o(m)都是某个适当可学习问题的必要误差水平；(3) 正则化不是通用的学习原理——存在无法由任何SRM学习器学习的适当可学习类，以及无法由任何局部正则化器学习的可学习类。此外还给出SRM可学习性的两个充分条件及SRM可表示性的刻画。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：学习不能通过扩大假设类归约为适当学习，适当学习本质上可能需要训练误差（且这种需求在任意次线性标度上都会出现），正则化（SRM或局部正则化器）亦无法作为通用学习器。这解决了三个开放问题，并确立了适当学习与正则化各自的能力边界。与此同时，正面结果（SRM可学习性的充分条件及其通过显示偏好可积性的刻化）为理解何时正则化足够提供了一个理论框架。

Abstract: Two of the most fundamental questions in statistical learning theory are the following: which prediction problems are learnable, and how should they be learned? For the former, elegant answers often take the form of combinatorial dimensions. The latter question, however, has proved considerably more elusive: all known general-purpose multiclass learners rely on intricate orientations of exponentially large one-inclusion structures, and familiar algorithmic principles such as proper learning and regularization remain poorly understood. Motivated by prior work, we ask whether learning reduces to proper learning---possibly over a larger hypothesis class---and whether proper or improper multiclass learning can ultimately be captured by suitable regularizers.
  Our primary results answer both questions negatively, resolving three open problems from prior work. First, we exhibit a learnable multiclass problem that cannot be embedded in any properly learnable class, meaning learning cannot be reduced to proper learning by enlarging the hypothesis class. Second, we demonstrate that proper learning can require training error and characterize this phenomenon precisely: every properly learnable class admits a proper learner making $o(m)$ errors on samples of size $m$, but every prescribed sublinear scale $a_m=o(m)$ is necessary for some properly learnable problem. Third, regularization is not a general learner: we exhibit a properly learnable class that cannot be learned by any Structural Risk Minimization (SRM) learner, and a learnable class that cannot be learned by any local regularizer. We complement these impossibility results with a positive theory that gives two sufficient conditions for SRM learnability and characterizes SRM representability through integrability of revealed preferences.

</details>


### [85] [High Probability Derivative Bounds for Random tanh Neural Networks on a Hypercube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26526)
*Josef Dick,Michael Feischl,Fabian Zehetgruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对激活函数导数满足阶乘增长界的宽随机神经网络，建立了输入混合导数的高概率上界，并特化到Xavier初始化的tanh网络，证明其深度依赖可从指数级改进为多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于权矩阵Euclid算子范数的确定性分析给出的导数界通常随深度呈指数增长，但这可能过于悲观。作者希望在足够宽的Gaussian网络中显著改善该增长，从而为网络正则性（Lipschitz常数、加权Sobolev范数）提供更好的高概率刻画，并服务于拟蒙特卡洛（QMC）积分与基于QMC的训练分析。

Method: 作者将最高阶导数中线性相关的项分离出来，并通过可测有限网（measurable finite nets）控制对应的切方向，从而在足够宽的 Gaussian 网络假设下改进深度依赖。关键估计利用激活导数满足阶乘增长界的假设，结合宽度条件 n ≥ C(L^3 n0^2(1+log n0) + L^2(1+log(L/η)))，以概率至少 1-η 同时控制所有非空指标集 u⊆[n0] 与输入 x∈[0,1]^{n0} 上的混合导数。

Result: 对Xavier初始化的标量输出tanh网络，存在常数 C,C0,C1>0，在宽度满足上述条件时，以概率至少 1-η 有 |D^u R_{Φ^(L)}(x)| ≤ C0 |u|! (C1 L)^{|u|-1} ∏_{j∈u} β_j(η,n0) 对所有非空 u 与 x 同时成立。由此一阶导数界不依赖深度，平方自由的 |u| 阶混合导数至多以 L^{|u|-1} 多项式增长（除坐标因子外）。作为推论，得到网络实现的Euclid Lipschitz常数与加权Sobolev范数的高概率界。

Conclusion: 该结果说明宽随机tanh网络的导数正则性随深度仅呈多项式退化而非指数退化，且这些导数估计可直接与拟蒙特卡洛积分相联系，表明此类正则性可被纳入基于QMC的训练分析中。

Abstract: We establish high-probability bounds for mixed input derivatives of wide random neural networks whose activation derivatives satisfy a factorial growth bound. Our main result specializes these estimates to $\tanh$ networks with Xavier initialization. A direct deterministic analysis based on Euclidean operator norms of the weight matrices yields derivative bounds that generally grow exponentially with the depth. We show that this growth can be substantially improved for sufficiently wide Gaussian networks by isolating the term that is linear in the highest-order derivative and controlling the corresponding tangent directions by measurable finite nets.
  For scalar-output $\tanh$ networks with Gaussian weights and Xavier initialization, we prove that there exist constants $C,C_0,C_1>0$ such that, whenever the common hidden width satisfies $n \geq C\left(L^3n_0^2(1+\log n_0)+L^2\left(1+\log(L/η)\right)\right)$, then, with probability at least $1-η$, the estimate $\left|D^u\mathcal{R}_{Φ^{(L)}}(x)\right| \leq C_0 |u|! (C_1L)^{|u|-1}\prod_{j\in u}β_j(η,n_0)$ holds simultaneously for every non-empty $u\subseteq[n_0]$ and every $x\in[0,1]^{n_0}$. Thus, the first-order derivative bound is independent of the depth, while a square-free mixed derivative of order $|u|$ grows at most polynomially as $L^{|u|-1}$, apart from the coordinate factors. As consequences, we obtain high-probability bounds for the Euclidean Lipschitz constant and for weighted Sobolev norms of the network realization. The latter connect the derivative estimates to quasi-Monte Carlo integration and indicate how such regularity can enter the analysis of QMC-based training.

</details>


### [86] [Predicting Quantifiability from Primary Screens to Prioritize Dose-Response Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26538)
*Sean Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"可量化性"（quantifiability）——即后续剂量-反应实验能否产出可用效价估计——作为区别于生物活性的独立分诊目标进行建模。研究发现可量化性可由前期的低成本筛选数据强预测，主要预测信息来自筛选观测特征而非分子结构，且响应类预测因子在未见过化学骨架和新测定机制家族上保持稳健。该发现表明实验可测性是可预测的筛选结果属性，可量化性感知的分诊能改进昂贵剂量-反应分析能力的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量药物筛选依赖低成本初筛来优先选择化合物进入更昂贵的剂量-反应分析（其中效价最终被量化）。现有筛选策略主要识别能在后续确认生物学活性的化合物，隐含假设已确认的活性必然能产生可用效价估计。然而，已确认的生物活性并不总能转化为可量化的效价——活性化合物仍可能无法产出可报告的剂量-反应估计。因此需要将可量化性作为与生物活性不同的独立分诊目标进行建模。

Method: 作者构建了一个专门建模可量化性的框架，将"后续测试能否产出可用效价估计"作为独立的二分类预测目标。他们从前期的低成本筛选数据中提取特征，评估预测信息主要来自筛选观测特征还是分子结构，并在未见过化学骨架（泛化到新支架）与留出的测定机制家族上检验响应类预测因子的稳健性，同时分析成功量化的概率如何随响应幅度和测定情境变化。

Result: 可量化性可从先前的低成本筛选中强预测，且大多数预测信息来自观测到的筛选特征而非分子结构。基于响应的预测因子在之前未见过的化学支架上仍然稳健，并能泛化到留出的测定机制家族；成功量化的概率随响应幅度和测定情境变化强烈。

Conclusion: 这些发现确立了实验可测性（区别于生物活性）是筛选结果的可预测属性，并表明可量化性感知的分诊可以改善昂贵剂量-反应分析能力的分配，为筛选流程优化提供新的决策依据。

Abstract: High-throughput drug screening relies on low-cost primary assays to prioritize compounds for more expensive dose-response profiling, where potency is ultimately quantified. Current screening strategies largely focus on identifying compounds that will confirm biological activity on follow-up, implicitly assuming that confirmed activity will also yield a usable potency estimate. However, confirmed biological activity in screening does not necessarily translate into a quantifiable potency, because active compounds can still fail to produce a reportable dose-response estimate. We therefore present a framework for modeling quantifiability, whether follow-up testing will yield a usable potency estimate, as a distinct triage objective from biological activity. Quantifiability was strongly predictable from the preceding low-cost screen, with most predictive information arising from the observed screening features rather than molecular structure. Response-based predictors remained robust on previously unseen chemical scaffolds and generalized across held-out assay-mechanism families, while the probability of successful quantification varied strongly with response amplitude and assay context. These findings establish experimental measurability, distinct from biological activity, as a predictable property of screening outcomes and show that quantifiability-aware triage can improve the allocation of costly dose-response profiling capacity.

</details>


### [87] [Chart2SVG: Editable SVG Generation from Raster Chart Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26544)
*Jinning Cui,Lu Chen,Haoyan Shi,Yue He,Chenglong Wang,Mengyu Zhou,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chart2SVG 是一个多模态大语言模型，能将静态栅格图表转换为结构组织良好、语义丰富的 SVG 格式，支持编程式编辑。它通过图表专用语义 token 捕获几何图元及其功能角色，并提出 Beagle+ 数据集（33K 规范化与结构蒸馏样本），结合专门训练目标与渲染感知后训练阶段，生成视觉准确且结构一致的 SVG。此外还构建了图表结构图（CSG）以暴露视觉依赖关系，支持交互式探索、图表重用与布局复用。实验表明其在重建保真度和下游编辑效用上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态栅格图表难以进行程序化编辑和下游重用，缺乏结构与语义信息。现有方法难以同时恢复图表的几何图元及其功能角色，导致重建结果在结构一致性和可编辑性上不足，阻碍了智能、交互式可视化工具的构建。

Method: 1) 将图表专用语义 token 融入视觉-语言模型，同时建模几何图元与功能角色；2) 构建 Beagle+ 数据集（33K 规范化与结构蒸馏的图表样本）支撑结构恢复训练；3) 采用专门训练目标并结合渲染感知的后训练阶段，兼顾视觉保真与结构一致性；4) 构建图表结构图（CSG）显式建模视觉依赖关系，支持更高层级的操作。

Result: 实验表明 Chart2SVG 在重建保真度和下游编辑效用方面大幅超越基线方法，证明了其在结构恢复与可编辑性上的有效性。

Conclusion: Chart2SVG 通过语义 token 与渲染感知训练实现了高质量的结构化 SVG 图表重建，CSG 进一步解锁了交互探索、图表重利用与布局复用等高级功能，推进了智能交互式可视化工具的发展。

Abstract: We present Chart2SVG, a multimodal large language model that converts static raster charts into structurally organized, semantically enriched SVGs that support programmatic editing. By incorporating chart-specific semantic tokens into a vision-language model, Chart2SVG captures both geometric primitives and their functional roles. To support robust structural recovery, we introduce Beagle+, a dataset of 33K canonicalized and structurally distilled chart samples. Our approach combines specialized training objectives with a rendering-aware post-training phase, producing SVGs that are both visually accurate and structurally consistent. To facilitate higher-level manipulations, we construct a Chart Structure Graph (CSG) that exposes visual dependencies, enabling tasks such as interactive exploration, chart repurposing, and layout reuse. Experiments show that Chart2SVG substantially outperforms baselines in reconstruction fidelity and downstream editing utility, advancing the development of intelligent and interactive visualization tools.

</details>


### [88] [Arrive and Survive: Scaling Safe Goal-Conditioned Policy Learning from One-Bit Failure Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26571)
*Guopeng Li,Yiyang Duan,Yiru Jiao,Chengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在失败终止的马尔可夫决策过程中，现有对比强化学习(CRL)在构建正样本时忽略了失败终止所移除的概率质量，导致目标达成价值被系统性高估，近失败轨迹会错误地强化不安全动作。作者提出Safe-CRL，仅利用失败终止的一比特信号，通过两个最小修正（mass-weighted InfoNCE纠正评论家学习中短存活未来的过度加权，log-survival-mass score在策略优化中恢复缺失的存活质量），在12个易失败机器人导航与运动任务上显著提升生存率与目标达成性能，并展示出复杂的避障行为，完善了失败终止下的CRL理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比强化学习(CRL)在目标条件任务中通过自监督对比目标有效扩展策略学习，但在失败终止的MDP中，既有CRL仅将失败前未来目标视为正样本，未考虑失败终止所移除的概率质量。理论分析表明这一遗漏会造成目标达成价值的系统性高估偏差，使近失败轨迹提供不成比例的成功监督，从而通过灾难性失败自举强化不安全动作，导致策略学习失败与不可持续的追求目标行为。因此需要纠正该偏差以实现安全的可扩展目标条件策略学习。

Method: 提出Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL)，包含两个最小但强力的修正：(1) mass-weighted InfoNCE，用于纠正评论家学习中对短存活未来的过度加权；(2) log-survival-mass score，用于在策略优化中恢复缺失的存活概率质量。该方法仅依赖失败终止提供的一比特信号，不引入额外监督。

Result: 在12个易失败的机器人导航与运动任务中，Safe-CRL一致地提升了生存率，并在目标达成性能上大幅超越Scaling-CRL基线；深度Safe-CRL策略还展现出复杂的避障/失败规避行为。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了失败终止条件下的CRL理论，并提供了一个可扩展的安全强化学习框架，验证了仅凭失败终止信号即可实现安全的目标条件策略学习。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) scales effectively in goal-conditioned tasks by casting policy learning into a self-supervised contrastive objective. However, in a failure-terminated Markov decision process, established CRL considers pre-failure future goals only when constructing positive samples, without accounting for the probability mass removed by failure termination. Our theoretical analysis shows that this omission induces a systematic overestimation bias in goal-reaching values. Consequently, near-failure trajectories provide disproportionately strong supervision of success despite retaining little future occupancy. Unsafe actions can thereby be reinforced through catastrophic failure bootstrapping, leading to failed policy learning and unsustainable goal-reaching behaviours. To address this problem, we introduce two minimal yet strong corrections: mass-weighted InfoNCE corrects the overweighting of short surviving futures in critic learning, and a log-survival-mass score restores the missing survival mass in policy optimization. The resulting method, Safe Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (Safe-CRL), requires only the one-bit signal provided by failure termination to scale safe goal-conditioned policy learning. Across twelve failure-prone robot navigation and locomotion tasks, Safe-CRL consistently improves survival and substantially outperforms the Scaling-CRL baseline in goal-reaching performance. Additionally, deep Safe-CRL policies exhibit complex failure-avoidance behaviours. This study completes the CRL theory under failure termination and provides a scalable safe RL framework. The code is available via https://github.com/RomainLITUD/safe-crl.

</details>


### [89] [Activation Outliers Matter: Robust Recovery for Quantized Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26581)
*Tanzila Rahman,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Zhuang Ma,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Yao Wang,Xing Huang,Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Yonghan Dong,Wei Guo,Yaoyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多模态大模型（MLLM）中从MXFP8到MXFP4/HiF4等低比特量化方案，发现MXFP8近似无损而4比特量化显著退化，且激活量化是性能损失的主要来源；据此提出残差回退量化（RFQ），通过辅助量化残差通路对超低比特激活表示进行轻量重建，在不修改架构、几乎零开销的情况下，在Wan2.2和Qwen3-VL上显著恢复了MXFP4/HiF4量化损失的精度，大幅缩小与BF16基线的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化是降低MLLM计算与内存需求的有效途径，且硬件已支持MXFP8直至MXFP4、HiF4等超低比特格式。但现有研究缺乏对这些量化方案在覆盖视频生成与推理任务的代表性MLLM上的系统性分析，也尚未明确超低比特量化下性能损失的根本来源，这限制了对4比特高效部署的指导。

Method: 作者首先对MXFP8、MXFP4、HiF4等量化方案在视频生成与推理两类MLLM上进行系统评测与消融实验，定位性能瓶颈；随后提出Residual Fallback Quantization（RFQ）：在原始超低比特激活表示之外附加一条量化的残差通路，显式建模并补偿量化误差，从而提升激活保真度。RFQ无需架构改动，额外计算开销可忽略。

Result: 实验表明：MXFP8达到接近无损的性能，而4比特量化导致显著退化；消融证明激活量化是性能损失的主导因素，贡献远超权重量化。在Wan2.2与Qwen3-VL上的大量实验显示，RFQ在MXFP4和HiF4量化下持续恢复大部分损失精度，在生成与推理基准上大幅缩小与BF16基线的差距。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了激活量化是超低比特MLLM性能退化中的主导瓶颈，并证明基于残差的激活重建是一种实用、有效的4比特部署策略，为MLLM低比特量化研究提供了明确的方向性结论与可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization offers a promising avenue for reducing the computational and memory demands of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Recent hardware support for low-precision formats, ranging from MXFP8 to ultra-low-bit formats such as MXFP4 and HiF4, has accelerated research into efficient MLLM training and deployment. In this work, we present a systematic study of these quantization schemes in representative MLLMs that span both video generation and reasoning tasks. Our analysis shows that MXFP8 achieves near-lossless performance, whereas aggressive 4-bit quantization leads to significant degradation. Through extensive ablations, we identify activation quantization as the primary source of this performance loss, contributing substantially more than weight quantization. Motivated by this observation, we propose Residual Fallback Quantization (RFQ), a lightweight activation reconstruction framework that supplements the primary ulta-low-bit activation representation with an auxiliary quantized residual pathway. By explicitly modeling and compensating for quantization errors, RFQ improves activation fidelity while preserving the efficiency advantages of ultra-low-bit computation. RFQ requires no architectural modifications and incurs negligible computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Wan2.2 and Qwen3-VL demonstrate that RFQ consistently recovers a substantial portion of the performance lost under the quantization of MXFP4 and HiF4, significantly narrowing the gap to BF16 baselines across both generation and 4 reasoning benchmarks. Our findings establish activation quantization as the dominant bottleneck in ultra-low-bit MLLMs and highlight residual-based activation reconstruction as an effective and practical strategy for robust 4-bit deployment.

</details>


### [90] [J-Zero: Unified Challenger--Solver--Judge Co-Evolution from Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26582)
*Gyouk Chu,Myeongho Jeon,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: J-Zero 是一个统一的 Challenger–Solver–Judge 协同进化框架，可在可验证与不可验证两类领域实现无需标注数据的模型自我进化。挑战者与求解者通过对抗交互协同进化，而评判者则利用“响应产生方式本身决定序”的偏好对进行联合适应，而非依赖自身打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我进化语言模型有望降低人类监督成本，是通向超级智能的潜在路径；但已有进展多集中在可验证领域，不可验证领域的自我进化研究明显不足。因此需要一种能同时覆盖两类领域的统一框架。

Method: 提出 J-Zero 框架，包含 Challenger、Solver、Judge 三个角色协同进化。Challenger 与 Solver 通过对抗交互共同进化：Challenger 生成难度递增的任务，Solver 学习产出更高质量的答案。与此同时，Judge 基于“先后顺序已知”的偏好对进行联合适应（例如 Solver 的答案优于 Challenger 的答案；Solver 分解再重组后的答案优于其一击式答案），而非依据 Judge 自身评分，由此实现从零数据出发的自我改进。

Result: 在可验证领域平均超越基线 4.2 分，在不可验证领域平均超越基线 8.0 分；并且至少在十轮迭代中持续提升，而基线方法在两轮之后即开始退化。

Conclusion: J-Zero 验证了无需人工监督数据、仅靠框架内部角色协同进化即可在可验证与不可验证领域实现持续自我提升，补齐了不可验证领域自我进化研究不足的缺口，并为更通用的模型自我进化提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving language models have recently emerged as a promising path toward superintelligence, with the advantage of reducing the cost of human supervision. While considerable progress has been made in verifiable domains, self-evolution in unverifiable domains remains substantially less explored. We propose Judge co-adaptation from Zero data (J-Zero), a unified Challenger--Solver--Judge co-evolution framework that supports self-improvement across both domains. The Challenger and Solver co-evolve through an adversarial interaction: the Challenger generates increasingly difficult tasks, while the Solver learns to produce higher-quality responses to them. In parallel, the Judge co-adapts using preference pairs whose ordering is known in advance from how each response was produced, i.e., the Solver's answer over the Challenger's, and its decomposed-and-recombined answer over its one-shot answer, rather than from the Judge's own scores. J-Zero outperforms the baselines by an average of 4.2 points on verifiable and 8.0 points on unverifiable domains, and continues to improve through at least ten iterations, whereas the baselines degrade after two.

</details>


### [91] [GRAS: Guided Reduced-Variance Proposals and Adaptive Selection for Training-Free Reward Alignment in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26585)
*Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAS 是一种针对离散扩散模型的无训练推理期奖励引导方法，通过改进引导提议（Rao-Blackwellized reveal 与 leave-one-out baseline 降低方差）和搜索粒子重采样（自适应温度，被证明等价于单一主动成分），在调控 DNA 与蛋白质设计任务上取得最优无训练奖励，甚至匹配或超越奖励微调模型，并对不可微奖励同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已成为序列数据的主流生成器，在推理时无需重新训练即可将其引导至下游奖励越来越重要。现有的无训练引导常结合梯度引导与搜索两种机制，但作者识别出两个弱点：引导提议仅从单个带噪样本估计梯度（方差高），且搜索在固定温度下重采样粒子，忽略了奖励在每个去噪步骤的分布。这促使作者设计一套零额外去噪成本的改进方案。

Method: GRAS 由两部分组成：在提议端，对可微奖励采用 Rao-Blackwellized reveal 降低梯度估计方差，对不可微奖励采用 leave-one-out baseline；在搜索端，将每步值标准化为组相对优势（group-relative advantage），并证明其坍缩为一个单一主动成分——自适应重采样温度。整体不增加任何去噪器计算成本。

Result: 在调控 DNA 与蛋白质设计任务上，GRAS 取得了最优的无训练奖励性能，优于先前所有无训练方法，并匹配或超越奖励微调模型；且对非可微奖励依然保持有效。

Conclusion: GRAS 表明：通过降低提议方差与自适应化搜索温度这两项简单改动，可以显著提升训练自由引导的离散扩散模型奖励，且无需额外去噪成本，甚至在不可微奖励场景下也能达到接近微调模型的性能。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have become a strong, widely adopted class of generators for sequence data, and steering them toward a downstream reward at inference time, without any retraining, is increasingly important. Such training-free steering is done by gradient guidance, by search, or by combining the two. We study the combined regime and identify two weaknesses in how it is usually run: the guided proposal estimates its gradient from a single noisy sample, and the search then resamples particles at a fixed temperature that ignores how rewards spread across each denoising step. We address both with a small set of changes that add no denoiser cost. For the proposal, we lower the estimator variance with a Rao-Blackwellized reveal for differentiable rewards and a leave-one-out baseline for non-differentiable ones; for the search, we standardize the per-step values into a group-relative advantage and prove it collapses to a single active ingredient, an adaptive resampling temperature. We call the resulting method Guided Reduced-variance proposals and Adaptive Selection (GRAS). GRAS is simple yet effective: across regulatory DNA and protein design it attains the best training-free reward, outperforming prior training-free methods and matching or surpassing a reward-fine-tuned model, and it remains effective even for non-differentiable rewards.

</details>


### [92] [SimCast-S2S: An Efficient Generative Model for Subseasonal Precipitation Forecasting via Transfer Learning from Climate Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26594)
*Hiep V. Dang,Antonios Mamalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimCast-S2S 是一个基于生成式潜在扩散框架的概率性子季节到季节（S2S）降水预报系统，通过三个关键设计——扩散生成流程用于不确定性量化、变分自编码器（VAE）的紧凑潜在空间用于高效生成大集合、以及低秩适应（LoRA）的模拟到再分析迁移学习解决训练数据不足问题——在再分析数据上超越深度学习基线，并在无后处理、偏差校正或校准的情况下与 ECMWF-S2S 等业务系统竞争乃至超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S 降水预报具有重大经济和社会影响，但因预测信号弱、不确定性高以及业务系统计算成本高昂（限制模拟保真度）而极具挑战。现有数据驱动方法在不确定性量化、大集合生成的计算效率以及训练数据规模方面存在三大瓶颈，亟需一种高效、可扩展的概率性预报方案。

Method: SimCast-S2S 采用生成式潜在扩散框架，具体包含：(1) 首次在 S2S 预测中使用基于扩散的生成流水线以有效采样条件分布，实现不确定性量化；(2) 通过变分自编码器学习紧凑潜在空间，在低维表示中高效生成大规模集合，避免物理空间中的高昂计算代价；(3) 采用迁移学习与低秩适应（LoRA），先在大规模气候模拟集合上预训练，再在有限的再分析数据上微调，克服扩散模型对大型训练数据的需求。

Result: 在再分析数据上，SimCast-S2S 优于包括卷积神经网络和 U-Net 架构在内的深度学习基线。值得注意的是，尽管仅使用部分大气输入变量且无后处理、偏差校正或校准，SimCast-S2S 仍可与 ECMWF-S2S 等最先进的业务系统相竞争，并在许多情形下表现更优。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，潜在生成建模与模拟到再分析迁移学习的结合，为数据驱动的概率性 S2S 降水预报提供了一条高效且可扩展的路径，在保持业务竞争力的同时大幅降低计算资源需求。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) precipitation forecasting has substantial financial and societal impact, yet remains challenging because of weak predictive signals, high associated uncertainty, and the computational cost of operational systems, which constrains simulation fidelity. We introduce SimCast-S2S, a generative latent-diffusion framework for probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting that addresses three major bottlenecks in data-driven prediction. First, because S2S prediction requires uncertainty quantification rather than only deterministic point forecasts, SimCast-S2S is the first data-driven system that uses a diffusion-based generative pipeline for S2S prediction, enabling effective sampling from the underlying conditional distribution. Second, since generating large probabilistic ensembles is computationally costly in physical space, SimCast-S2S instead operates in a compact latent space learned by variational autoencoders, enabling efficient large-ensemble generation. Third, diffusion models typically require large training datasets; SimCast-S2S overcomes this via transfer learning with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), pretraining on large ensembles of climate simulations before fine-tuning on limited reanalysis data. On reanalysis data, SimCast-S2S outperforms deep learning baselines, including convolutional neural networks and U-Net architectures. Notably, despite using only a subset of atmospheric input variables and no post-processing, bias correction, or calibration, SimCast-S2S remains competitive with, and in many cases outperforms, state-of-the-art operational systems such as the ECMWF-S2S baseline. These results indicate that latent generative modeling combined with simulation-to-reanalysis transfer learning offers an efficient and scalable path toward data-driven probabilistic S2S precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [93] [Technical Comparative Benchmarking Study: Advanced AI Hybrid Methods for Renewable Energy Farm Optimization and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26613)
*Majid Masoumi,Asghar Dashtiy,Mohammad Dehghan,Mina Rajabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对多种人工智能模型（传统ML、集成学习、深度神经网络、循环架构、Transformer、图模型及混合集成深度学习）在互补的可再生能源场景下进行了全面基准测试，涵盖大规模WEC数据集、16台WEC数据集以及Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA实测数据。结果表明：结构化WEC布局数据中Extra Trees表现最强（相对MLP基线MAE降低约63.7%）；STGCN通过显式学习时空交互将风功率MAE降至约167.0 kW（R=0.93）；整体最佳精度由RF BiLSTM混合模型取得（MAE=150.5 kW，较独立LSTM降低约75%，较STGCN提升约10%）。最终结论为不存在普遍最优的单一AI架构，模型选择取决于数据结构特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可再生能源预测领域，尽管各类AI架构（传统机器学习、集成学习、深度学习、图神经网络、混合模型）被广泛使用，但缺乏在互补场景下对这些方法进行系统、公平的横向对比的研究。作者旨在回答"哪种架构在何种数据条件下最优"这一关键问题，为实际部署提供选型依据。

Method: 采用系统性基准测试框架，在三类数据集（大规模WEC结构数据、16台WEC数据、Penmanshiel风电场10分钟SCADA运营数据）上对比七类模型族：传统ML、集成学习（如Extra Trees）、深度神经网络、循环架构（LSTM）、Transformer、图模型（STGCN）以及混合集成深度学习（如RF BiLSTM）。通过统一的MAE与相关系数R等指标评估各模型在不同数据形态（结构化布局、时空交互、非线性表格关系与时间动态并存）下的预测性能。

Result: 实验发现：1) 对结构化WEC布局数据，随机划分与提升机制的树集成（Extra Trees最强）显著优于常规ML和神经网络，相对MLP基线MAE降低约63.7%；2) 风功率预测中，STGCN通过显式学习空间与时间上的涡轮交互，将MAE降至约167.0 kW并取得R=0.93；3) 整体最佳为RF BiLSTM混合模型，MAE=150.5 kW，较独立LSTM降低约75%，较STGCN进一步改善约10%。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一AI架构在所有情境下普遍最优：随机化树集成最适合结构化WEC代理建模，图网络在显式空间交互占主导时更具优势，集成循环混合模型则在非线性表格关系与时间动态并存时提供最强平衡。模型选择应匹配数据的底层结构与任务特性。

Abstract: This study provides a comprehensive benchmarking of conventional machine learning (ML), ensemble learning, deep neural networks, recurrent architectures, Transformers, graph based models, and hybrid ensemble deep learning approaches under complementary renewable energy scenarios. Three datasets are considered: a large scale WEC dataset, a 16 WEC dataset, and operational 10 min SCADA measurements at the Penmanshiel wind farm. For structured WEC layout data, tree ensembles exhibited a clear advantage over conventional ML and neural predictors because randomized partitioning and boosting efficiently captured nonlinear layout power interactions without requiring explicit feature representation learning. The Extra Trees was the strongest model, achieving considerable results. Relative to the MLP baseline, this corresponds to an approximately 63.7% reduction in MAE, demonstrating the suitability of randomized tree ensembles for high dimensional structured WEC data. Also, STGCN reduced the MAE to approximately 167.0 kW and achieved R = 0.93 by explicitly learning spatial and temporal turbine interactions. The best overall forecasting accuracy was obtained by the RF BiLSTM hybrid, with an MAE=150.5 kW. Compared with standalone LSTM, this represents an approximately 75% reduction in MAE, while improving on STGCN by approximately 10.0%. Finally, the experiments reveal that no single AI architecture is universally optimal: randomized and boosted ensembles are particularly effective for structured WEC surrogate modeling, graph networks become advantageous when explicit spatial interactions dominate, and ensemble recurrent hybrids provide the strongest balance when nonlinear tabular relationships and temporal dynamics coexist.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EduRiskX，一个神经符号框架，结合基于时间Transformer的预测器与F-Logic符号推理，用于在线教育学生学业风险的早期检测与可解释预测。在OULAD数据集上（80/10/10学生级划分）实现学期末（第38周）准确率0.900、F1-score 0.894，平均早期检测周为9.32周，检测率达94.30%，优于PatchTST、iTransformer等SOTA时间序列模型，并通过F-Logic规则提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学生学业风险预测模型存在早期检测能力有限与可解释性不足的问题，导致"黑箱"信任危机，阻碍其在真实教学场景中的落地应用。作者希望构建一个既能早期识别风险、又能提供可解释诊断依据的框架，以支持及时干预、提升学生保留率与学习效果。

Method: EduRiskX为神经符号（neuro-symbolic）框架，包含两部分：1）神经组件——基于时间Transformer，利用时间注意力建模纵向学生活动序列，并引入类加权损失与动态周截断；2）符号组件——基于教育理论（参与理论Engagement Theory与学生整合模型Student Integration Model）构建F-Logic规则库，模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑，且仅从训练数据构造，充当数据驱动专家系统。最终通过基于逻辑回归的融合机制，学习神经风险概率与符号置信分数的相对贡献来综合预测。

Result: 在Open University Learning Analytics Dataset（OULAD）上的严格80/10/10学生级划分实验中，EduRiskX学期末（第38周）准确率达0.900，F1-score为0.894，平均早期检测周为9.32，检测率94.30%。与SOTA时间序列模型（PatchTST、iTransformer）及常见深度学习基线（LSTM、CNN）相比，在相同条件下取得了更好的召回率与更早的风险识别。

Conclusion: EduRiskX在预测性能与可解释性之间取得了良好平衡：不仅能更早、更准确地检测学业风险，还通过F-Logic模块提供结构化的基于规则的解释，将预测结果与可观察的行为模式及教育理论联系起来，增强了模型的透明度和可信度，有助于推动其在真实教学场景中的应用。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [95] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了独立大语言模型（LLM）与四步智能体流水线在ICU死亡率解释生成中的表现。基于2,353例ICU住院的eICU Demo数据集，XGBoost预测模型AUROC为0.855；在38例分层解释子集中，独立LLM出现1例显式结局泄漏，智能体流水线为0例。独立LLM在SHAP对齐和方向一致性上更高，而智能体流水线在指南依据、数值特异性和合理度上更优，提示智能体分解可提升安全相关的依据支撑，但需搭配归因校验使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型能准确预测ICU死亡率，但仅靠特征归因方法难以提供临床所需的叙述性解释。大语言模型有望弥合这一差距，多步智能体流水线通过分离数据解释、指南核查和最终解释，成为有前景的扩展方案。

Method: 保留原始"独立模型vs智能体流水线"对比设计：以XGBoost预测模型（AUROC 0.855, AUPRC 0.332）为基础，在38例分层解释子集上比较独立LLM与四步智能体流水线生成的解释，评估显式结局泄漏、SHAP对齐（平均Jaccard）、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性和合理度等指标。

Result: 独立LLM产生1例显式结局泄漏，智能体流水线为0例。在14例与SHAP评审重叠的病例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；智能体流水线的指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）和合理度（0.700 vs 0.671）更高。

Conclusion: 智能体分解可能提升安全相关的指南依据和患者特异性细节，但在高风险风险解释场景中，应搭配基于归因的校验后再使用，不能单独依赖智能体流水线。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [96] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统综述了大模型（Large Models）在电池健康管理与故障预测（BPHM）中的应用，探讨Transformer架构、自监督预训练、多模态数据与PEFT等技术如何解决传统方法在计算效率、跨域泛化、数据依赖和可解释性方面的瓶颈，并提出了未来研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（基于物理模型和任务特定深度学习方法）存在计算效率低、参数化困难、跨域泛化能力差、依赖大量标注的Run-to-Failure数据以及模型可解释性不足等长期瓶颈，亟需新的范式来克服这些挑战。

Method: 文章首先阐述支撑大模型的基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集、PEFT技术），然后沿四个关键维度对近年进展进行分类：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识实现可解释性、实现系统级自动化。

Result: 综述表明大模型在BPHM各维度上展现出有前景的结果，但仍存在数据可获取性、智能验证、可信赖性和部署可行性等重大挑战。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一条研究路线图，聚焦于构建协作数据生态系统、面向工业应用的智能验证、基于物理信息设计的可信度提升，以及高效的端侧部署，为下一代电池管理系统的发展提供系统性指导。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [97] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PICasso，一个从自然语言规格说明自动完成光子集成回路（PIC）综合、验证与优化的 AI 辅助框架，并配套提出包含 36 个参数化设计任务的基准 PIC-Set。相比直接用大语言模型（LLM）生成，PICasso 显著提升端到端规格满足率（高复杂度电路的结构 Spec@3 最高达 92.7%，功能 Spec@3 达 52%），并通过仿真引导优化将平均插入损耗从 4.98 dB 降至 3.25 dB（改进 1.74 dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在芯片设计中的应用往往止步于生成"脆弱的网表"，缺乏对制造可行性、物理验证和性能优化等下游环节的闭环保障；同时缺少统一的评估协议和基准来衡量 AI 驱动的光子设计能力。作者旨在通过结构化领域约束、物理验证与仿真反馈，将 LLM 从"网表生成器"转变为真正可产出可制造版图的设计智能体。

Method: PICasso 采用结构化的 NL -> YAML -> GDS 生成流水线，结合 PDK 感知的知识注入、自动化布局布线、DRC/LVS 物理验证以及基于 SAX 的光子仿真。作者还构建了 PIC-Set 基准（36 个参数化设计任务，覆盖核心光子原语与多组件电路），并在统一评估协议下对多种 SOTA LLM 进行评测，引入结构/功能 Spec@k、优化效率、扰动鲁棒性等新指标。

Result: 在 PIC-Set 全基准上，PICasso 相比 vanilla LLM 生成显著提升端到端规格满足度：高复杂度电路的结构 Spec@3 最高 92.7%、功能 Spec@3 最高 52%。同时仿真引导优化持续降低插损，平均损耗从 4.98 dB 降到 3.25 dB（改善 1.74 dB），且运行时间可与基于 GUI 的手工流程竞争。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束、物理验证与仿真反馈能够将 LLM 从脆弱的网表生成器转化为实用的 PIC 设计智能体，能够产出满足制造要求（通过 DRC/LVS）且性能经过优化的版图，在质量与效率上均可匹敌甚至超越传统手工流程。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [98] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA，一个基于确定性工具的多智能体LLM框架，用于计算密集型的金融问答。该框架将语言理解与数值执行分离，通过专门智能体处理查询解析、路由、参数提取、计算规划和回复生成，并交由确定性Python工具执行金融计算与规则应用。在定期存款查询基准上达到95.54%的计算密集型准确率和90.87%的总体准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线，且使用17B开源骨干模型即可超越更大的前沿模型，表明架构设计比模型规模更决定数值可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算密集型的金融问答需要对结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束进行精确推理。尽管LLM在自然语言任务上表现强劲，但在求解多步金融计算时常产生数值上不正确但看似合理的答案，这是现有方法的主要局限。

Method: CIFQA采用确定性的工具接地（tool-grounded）多智能体LLM框架，将语言理解与数值执行分离。它指派专门的智能体分别负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和回复生成，而确定性的Python工具负责执行金融计算和规则应用。实例化于定期存款查询场景，并在精心构建的定期存款查询基准上进行评估。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%准确率，总体准确率90.87%，大幅超越直接LLM基线（即使基线获得完整公式、利率表和基准指令）。消融实验表明，精确利率查找、存期计算、滚动年度调整和提前支取逻辑等确定性组件是性能的关键贡献因素。值得注意的是，采用17B开源骨干模型的CIFQA优于在相同金融信息条件下评估的更大规模前沿模型。

Conclusion: 实验证明架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性。虽然目前仅针对定期存款查询进行评估，但CIFQA为计算密集型金融推理任务提供了一个可泛化的框架。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [99] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARL，一个基于自生成目标（autotelic）强化学习的闭环框架，用于探索和操控元胞自动机等复杂系统。与传统的开环方式（设置初始条件→运行完整模拟→观察结果）不同，CARL让智能体自主采样多样化目标，并学习目标条件化策略，通过最小、局部扰动在模拟过程中干预系统。在连续元胞自动机Lenia上实证展示了三种能力：高概率发现稳定孤子、学习操控既有孤子的运动方向、以及支持人类通过高层方向指令实时引导孤子穿越迷宫。训练出的智能体可零样本泛化到多种分布外条件，为自主或人机协作的"人工实验科学家"铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索元胞自动机及复杂系统的方法多为开环：设定初始条件、执行完整仿真、观察最终结果，过程中无法干预。这限制了系统性地发现和操控涌现现象的能力，缺乏一种能主动介入复杂系统演化过程的通用框架。

Method: 采用自生成强化学习（autotelic RL）构建闭环框架：智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习目标条件化策略，通过最少量的局部扰动干预复杂系统。在Lenia（连续元胞自动机）上实现为CARL系统，智能体跨越多种更新规则、目标和随机初始状态进行训练。

Result: 实验展示三项能力：（1）在广泛的Lenia更新规则下，CARL发现稳定孤子的比率高于启发式基线；（2）能用少量干预学习操控已有孤子的运动方向，证明其能控制而非仅创造自组织模式；（3）人类可借助训练好的智能体，通过高层方向指令实时引导孤子穿越迷宫，由智能体将指令翻译为低层干预。训练出的策略能够零样本泛化到多种分布外条件。

Conclusion: 结果表明闭环、目标驱动的方法能显著增强对涌现现象的发现与控制能力，为构建自主或受人类引导的"人工实验科学家"（artificial experimentalist agents）提供了可行路径，使其能发现并操控复杂系统中的涌现行为。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [100] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文聚焦于边缘环境（包括军事系统等关键任务场景）中的能量预测，提出并识别了“精度-效率悖论”：高精度的能量预测模型由于边缘AI的推理能耗与电池老化，反而可能造成净能量亏损。作者提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一视为能量损耗，用于最小化净能量损失，并在热敏感边缘环境中证明：复杂架构凭借更高精度所节省的能量，往往被其高运行强度造成的总能量损失所抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量预测的目标是最大化精度以提升能效、减少能量浪费，这一目标同样适用于关键任务边缘环境（如军事系统）。然而作者发现高精度预测模型会带来两个隐性代价——边缘AI的推理能耗和电池老化，导致高精度反而可能引发净能量赤字（Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox）。现有研究忽视了这两类能量损耗对“净收益”的影响，因此需要重新以系统化的成本视角审视预测模型的选择。

Method: 提出一个面向能量预测的总拥有成本（Total Cost of Ownership, TCO）框架，该框架将推理能量消耗和电池老化统一视为一种能量损失形式，理由是电池退化代表着系统未来能量承载能力的物理耗散。基于该框架对模型的精度收益与总能量成本进行综合权衡分析，并在热敏感边缘环境中进行评估。

Result: 研究表明，在热敏感边缘环境中，复杂架构凭借更高预测精度所节省的能量，往往被其高运行强度（推理能耗与老化加剧）造成的总能量损失所超过，从而验证了“精度-效率悖论”的存在以及TCO框架在揭示真实净能量收益方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 能量预测不应只追求精度最大化，而应从总能量损益（包括推理能耗与电池老化）出发进行模型选择；TCO框架为边缘AI能量预测提供了一种以最小化净能量损失为目标的评估与设计准则，对关键任务边缘系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss. This framework treats not only inference energy consumption but also battery aging as a unified form of energy loss, as degradation represents a physical dissipation of the system's future energy-carrying capacity. We demonstrate that in thermally sensitive edge environments, energy saved by the superior precision of complex architectures is often outweighed by the total energy lost through their high operational intensity.

</details>


### [101] [LLMs for Academic Workflows: An Evaluation of Literature Reviews Generated with Short and Long Context Windows of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26145)
*Muhammad Ali Chaudhry,Xinyuan Hao,Haifa Alwahaby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大型语言模型在短上下文与长上下文设置下生成的文献综述质量，由两名研究者对基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv文献生成的20篇AI文献综述在15个维度上进行评价。研究发现AI生成的文献综述需要人工监督才能达到学术出版标准，上下文窗口增大虽能纳入更广泛信息并维持连贯性，但也加剧了内容重复、关键工作遗漏以及偏重描述而非综合等问题。AI综述可提供基础性概览，但需领域专家批判性评估和润色，未来研究应考虑结合其他LLM和微调模型采取人机混合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究上下文窗口大小对AI生成文献综述质量的影响，以及AI在支持文献综述写作中所扮演的角色，旨在理解LLM在该学术任务中的能力边界与局限。

Method: 基于Semantic Scholar和Arxiv的研究来源，生成了20篇AI文献综述（涵盖短上下文与长上下文两种设置）；由两名研究者跨15个维度对这些综述进行系统评估和比较。

Result: AI生成的文献综述需人工监督方能达到学术出版标准；增大的上下文窗口可让LLM纳入更广泛的信息并保持长输入下的连贯性，但同时加剧了内容重复、关键文献遗漏以及偏重描述性而缺乏综合性分析等问题。

Conclusion: AI生成的综述可作为基础性概览，但输出必须由领域专家进行批判性评估与精炼。未来研究应探索在不同领域整合其他LLM和微调模型，采用结合人类专业知识和AI能力的混合方法，以解决本研究所识别出的局限。

Abstract: Our research focuses on evaluating literature reviews generated in short and long context settings of large language models (LLMs) to investigate the impact of context window on the quality of AI-generated literature reviews and the role of AI in supporting literature review writing. Twenty AI-generated literature reviews based on research sources from Semantic Scholar and Arxiv were evaluated by two researchers across 15 dimensions. Our findings reveal that AI-generated literature reviews require human oversight to meet academic publishing standards. As context windows increase, LLMs can incorporate broader information and maintain coherence across longer inputs, but they also exacerbate issues such as content repetition, omission of critical work, and a tendency towards descriptiveness over synthesis. Our work shows that AI-generated reviews can provide foundational overviews, but their output must be critically evaluated and refined by domain experts. Future research should consider integrating other LLMs and fine-tuned models in different domains with hybrid approaches that combine human expertise with AI capabilities to address the limitations identified in this study.

</details>


### [102] [Methodological and Conceptual Framework for 5D Multi-Table Analysis: A Unified Approach for Complex Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26149)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Hugo Kazzi,Aurélien Loison,Slim Hammadi,Emmanuel Chazard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于关系型（多表）数据学习的统一架构——关系超图Transformer（RHT）。它将关系数据库表示为超图，学习五维嵌入（PentE），并执行稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度与平均关系度成正比而非实体数量的平方。作者在Synthea合成电子健康记录数据集上，以每次就诊的SNOMED CT条件代码多标签预测为任务进行评测，与表格、关系型及时序图基线对比，结果表明RHT产生语义更连贯的嵌入且计算可扩展，尽管最高罕见代码召回率由XGBoost取得。论文还提供了消融研究和开源参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多表学习是医疗等复杂信息系统机器学习中的主要挑战：关系数据同时包含大体积数据、高维变量、高基数分类特征、复杂的表间依赖以及重复的时间观测。现有方法难以统一处理这些复杂度来源，因此需要一种能够刻画关系数据库中复杂依赖关系、同时保持计算可扩展性的统一架构。

Method: RHT将关系数据库建模为超图以捕获多元表间依赖；引入五维嵌入（PentE）表示实体；采用稀疏关系注意力机制，其复杂度定义为O(平均关系度×实体数)而非O(实体数²)，从而保证可扩展性。论文形式化定义了架构与注意力复杂度，并给出开源参考实现，同时报告各架构组件的消融研究。

Result: 在Synthea合成EHR数据集的SNOMED CT多标签预测基准上，RHT与表格、关系型及时序图基线相比，产生语义更连贯的嵌入且保持计算可扩展性；该基准中最高罕见代码召回率由XGBoost取得；消融实验量化了各架构组件的贡献。MIMIC-IV上的临床验证待PhysioNet认证后进行。

Conclusion: RHT展示了将关系数据库以超图和五维嵌入统一表示、并以稀疏注意力实现可扩展学习的可行性；其在嵌入语义连贯性上优于多种基线，但罕见代码预测性能仍不及XGBoost，提示未来需在标签稀缺场景下进一步改进。论文附有源码与实验协议以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Multi-table learning remains a major challenge in machine learning for healthcare and other complex information systems. Relational data combine several sources of complexity, including large data volume, high-dimensional variables, high-cardinality categorical features, complex inter-table dependencies, and repeated temporal observations. We introduce the Relational Hypergraph Transformer (RHT), a unified architecture that represents relational databases as hypergraphs, learns pentadimensional embeddings (PentE), and performs sparse relational attention with complexity proportional to the average relational degree rather than the square of the number of entities. We formally define the architecture, derive the complexity of its attention mechanism, and provide an open-source reference implementation. We evaluate RHT on the public Synthea synthetic electronic health record dataset using multi-label prediction of SNOMED CT condition codes per encounter, a task characterized by high categorical cardinality and long-tailed label distributions. Comparisons with tabular, relational, and temporal graph baselines show that RHT produces more semantically coherent embeddings while remaining computationally scalable. In this benchmark, the highest rare-code recall is achieved by XGBoost, whereas RHT attains the strongest embedding semantic coherence. We also report ablation studies quantifying the contribution of each architectural component. Clinical validation on MIMIC-IV is planned following PhysioNet credentialing. Source code and experimental protocols are provided in the accompanying repository.

</details>


### [103] [Leveraging Large Language Models for Systematic Literature Review of Disease Spread Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26150)
*Orhan Yagizer Cinar,Timur Emre Ozkose,Emma Von Hoene,Amira Roess,Taylor Anderson,Hamdi Kavak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的流水线，从536篇同行评审的基于代理建模（ABM）论文中提取模型相关信息，并与人工完成的系统文献综述（SLR）结果进行对比。结果显示，GPT-4.1的论文级准确率约为77.95%，GPT-5.0约为81.67%。字段级准确率介于32.40%到100.00%之间，复杂或主观字段表现较差。研究还发现，LLM之间的一致性可作为输出质量的潜在指标：低一致性可能意味着幻觉，而高一致性加上低准确率则可能指向人工数据集的噪声或错误。研究为提示工程提供了实践见解，并指出了LLM在建模与仿真领域全面SLR中的潜力和局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的飞速发展为自动化和简化研究流程（包括系统文献综述SLR）提供了新机遇。本文旨在探究LLM能否可靠地从大量学术论文中提取结构化信息，以替代或辅助耗时费力的人工SLR过程，并量化其准确性与局限性。

Method: 作者构建了一个LLM流水线，用于从536篇同行评审的基于代理建模论文中自动提取模型相关信息，并与人工完成的SLR结果进行系统对比。评估指标包括论文级准确率（GPT-4.1约77.95%，GPT-5.0约81.67%）和字段级准确率（范围32.40%至100.00%）。此外，通过分析两个LLM（GPT-4.1与GPT-5.0）输出之间的一致性，将其作为输出质量的潜在信号进行考察。

Result: 论文级准确率方面，GPT-5.0（81.67%）优于GPT-4.1（77.95%）。字段级准确率差异显著（32.40%~100.00%），表明复杂或主观的字段提取可靠性较低。LLM间一致性分析揭示了一个有趣的双向判断逻辑：低一致性可能指示幻觉，而高一致性结合低准确率则可能反映人工数据集本身存在噪声或标注错误。

Conclusion: LLM流水线在建模与仿真领域的大规模SLR中展现出较强潜力，尤其是在客观、明确的字段上表现可靠；但对于复杂或主观字段仍需人工介入。研究为LLM提示设计提供了实践指导，并强调利用LLM间一致性作为质量监控信号的价值，同时指出了当前技术用于全规模SLR的局限性。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have created new opportunities to streamline and potentially automate many research processes, including systematic literature reviews (SLRs). This study reports an LLM pipeline development for extracting model-relevant information from 536 peer-reviewed agent-based modeling papers. We compare the results with those of a human-conducted SLR. Our results show paper-level accuracies of approximately 77.95% for GPT-4.1 and 81.67% for GPT-5.0. Field-level accuracy ranges from 32.40% to 100.00%, with more complex or subjective fields performing less reliably. Importantly, we find that agreement between LLMs is a potential indicator of output quality: low agreement may signal hallucinations, whereas high agreement combined with low accuracy may point to noise or errors in the human dataset. Overall, our study provides practical insights into prompt development and highlights both the potential and limitations of using LLMs for full-scale SLRs in the modeling and simulation domain.

</details>


### [104] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Customer Churn Prediction in Telecommunications: A Framework for CRM Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26151)
*Sandeep Gaddamwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对电信客户流失预测模型可解释性不足、难以融入一线CRM工作流的问题，本文在IBM Telco流失数据集上基准测试了四种分类器（Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost、LightGBM），并提出一个四层CRM集成架构，将风险评分与SHAP/LIME归因向量转化为分层细分和保留行动模板。模型性能几乎持平（AUC差距仅0.011），LR获得最佳AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM获得最高准确率（78.42%）；聚焦最高风险五分之一客户的策略预计可降低整体流失3.3–5.3个百分点，每轮活动可保留约$199K–$319K收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信运营商的客户流失代价高昂（成熟市场月流失率约1.9%，年损失数十亿），预测模型虽能准确识别高风险客户，但集成与非线性模型的不透明导致一线留存专员无法仅凭概率分数设计个性化干预，模型因此被排斥在CRM工作流之外。本文旨在弥合"预测精度"与"可操作解释"之间的缺口。

Method: 在IBM Telco Customer Churn基准（7,043条记录、19个特征、26.5%流失率，仅训练集经SMOTE平衡至50%）上进行四种分类器的基准对比，采用5折交叉验证与AUC-ROC、准确率指标；提供全局SHAP排名（识别tenure、总费用、月付合同为主导流失信号）与实例级SHAP/LIME分解两种粒度的解释；并据此设计四层CRM集成架构：分层客户细分、特征到保留行动模板的映射、以及将活动结果回馈至再训练循环的闭环机制。

Result: Logistic Regression取得最强AUC-ROC（0.8411），LightGBM取得最高准确率（78.42%）；四种模型落在0.831–0.841的0.011 AUC窄带内，5折交叉验证表明领先模型实际上难分伯仲。面向最高风险五分之一群体的定向干预预计可将整体流失降低3.3–5.3个百分点，每轮活动周期可保留约$199K–$319K。

Conclusion: 可解释性可以在几乎不牺牲预测精度的情况下实现——表现最优的透明模型（Logistic Regression）与最强黑盒集成模型在统计上持平。通过全局与实例级SHAP/LIME解释，配合分层细分、行动模板和再训练反馈的四层CRM架构，可将流失预测转化为一线可操作的个性化保留策略，并带来可量化的营收与流失率改善。

Abstract: Subscriber attrition is a costly, persistent challenge for telecommunications providers, with monthly churn of roughly 1.9% in mature markets eroding billions in revenue annually. Predictive models can flag at-risk customers accurately, yet they are routinely excluded from frontline CRM workflows because high-performing ensemble and non-linear architectures are opaque: a retention specialist cannot design a personalised intervention from a probability score alone, without knowing why a subscriber is at risk. This paper addresses that gap. We benchmark four classifiers--Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM--on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (7,043 records; 19 features; 26.5% churn, balanced to 50% via SMOTE on the training partition only). Logistic Regression attains the strongest AUC-ROC (0.8411) and LightGBM the highest accuracy (78.42%); all four fall within a 0.011 AUC band (0.831--0.841), and 5-fold cross-validation confirms the leading models are effectively tied. Explanations are delivered at two granularities: a global SHAP ranking identifying tenure, total charges, and month-to-month contract as the dominant churn signals, and instance-level SHAP and LIME decompositions that expose the drivers behind each prediction. Building on these outputs, we introduce a four-layer CRM integration architecture that converts risk scores and attribution vectors into tiered segmentation, maps top features to structured retention-action templates, and routes campaign outcomes into a retraining feedback loop. Targeting the highest-risk quintile is projected to cut overall churn by 3.3--5.3 percentage points, preserving an estimated $199K--$319K per campaign cycle.

</details>


### [105] [EEG-to-Report: An Annotation and Feature-Text Framework for Training Language Models on Clinical EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26153)
